  content: |
      # ShayLock — strict Instagram screen-time blocker
      
      An Android prototype that limits how much Instagram (`com.instagram.android`) may be used per
      day. The user picks a **daily allowance** (10 s … 5 h); the app measures how much of that
      allowance is actually consumed and, once it is used up, a full-screen lock screen blocks
      Instagram until midnight.
      
      There is no PIN and no local bypass. Unlocking Instagram, pausing the block, changing the
      allowance, clearing today's usage and turning the blocker off all require a **one-time code from
      an accountability partner**. The code is requested through WhatsApp or SMS, expires after five
      minutes, works exactly once, and is held in memory only.
      
      Built with Kotlin, Jetpack Compose (Material 3), WorkManager and a service-based architecture. The
      UI is deliberately dark-only, with a progressive-disclosure setup flow and a focused active
      dashboard.
      
      ---
      
      ## 1. Requirements
      
      | Item | Version |
      |---|---|
      | Android Studio | Ladybug (2024.2) or newer |
      | JDK | 17 (bundled with Android Studio) |
      | Gradle / AGP | 8.11.1 / 8.10.0 (via the wrapper) |
      | compileSdk / targetSdk | 35 |
      | minSdk | 26 (Android 8.0) |
      | Device | Physical Android device, Android 8.0+ |
      
      `minSdk 26` is required because the overlay uses `TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY`, which only exists
      from API 26 onwards.
      
      ## 2. Build and install
      
      ### Android Studio
      1. `File > Open` and select the `ShayLock` folder (it is the Gradle root project).
      2. Let the Gradle sync finish.
      3. Connect the device over USB with **USB debugging** enabled.
      4. Press **Run** (Shift+F10) and pick the device from the device list.
      
      ### Command line
      ```powershell
      cd C:\NEXUS\ShayLock
      .\gradlew :app:assembleDebug
      adb install -r app\build\outputs\apk\debug\app-debug.apk
      ```
      
      The debug APK is written to `app\build\outputs\apk\debug\app-debug.apk`.
      
      ## 3. Grant the two system permissions
      
      The app cannot request these at runtime; they must be granted in system settings. The setup
      screen has a button for each one that jumps straight to the right settings page.
      
      1. **Accessibility service** — open ShayLock, tap *Open settings* on the
         "Accessibility service" row, find **ShayLock Blocker** in the *Installed services* list and
         switch it **on**, then confirm the warning dialog.
      2. **Display over other apps** — tap *Open settings* on the "Display over other apps" row and
         enable the switch for ShayLock.
      3. **Notifications** (optional, Android 13+) — tap *Allow*. The blocker works without it; the
         foreground-service notification and the protection health warning are simply hidden.
      
      The setup screen is progressive: it only shows the required permissions that are still missing,
      and the optional notification row is only offered during onboarding. Once both required
      permissions are active, the permission section collapses and the allowance picker appears. If the
      accessibility row shows an amber *Connecting…* state, wait a moment — the app re-checks
      automatically until the service is connected. Notifications can still be enabled later from the
      footer link.
      
      ## 4. Set up an accountability partner
      
      On the setup screen, tap **Accountability partner** and enter the partner's phone number with a
      country code (for example `+1 555 123 4567`).
      
      * The **first** partner can be saved directly.
      * Changing an **existing** partner requires a code approved by the current partner.
      * The number is stored locally in `SharedPreferences`; ShayLock itself has no backend and sends no
        data.
      
      The partner must be reachable in WhatsApp or through the device's SMS app. When an approval is
      needed, ShayLock opens that app with a pre-filled message containing the one-time code. The
      partner replies with the code, and the user types it into ShayLock.
      
      ## 5. Test it
      
      1. Pick a daily allowance (10 s / 15 min / 30 min / 1 h / 2 h / 3 h / 5 h) and press
         **Start blocking**. The very first setup needs **no partner approval** — the 10 s option is
         there to verify the whole flow quickly.
         → The allowance picker gives way to the active dashboard and the status card shows
         *"Protected / X left today"*.
      2. Change the allowance again → the approval dialog opens → tap **Request partner code** →
         WhatsApp or SMS opens with a message for the partner → enter the code the partner sends back.
      3. Open Instagram and leave it in the foreground for a while.
         → The status card shows a progress bar and *"X used / Y per day"*. The counter only advances
         while Instagram is really on screen.
      4. Keep going until the allowance is used up.
         → A full-screen dark lock screen appears over Instagram showing
         **"Instagram is blocked"** with a numeric keypad and four code dots.
      5. Press Back / Home / Recents → the overlay swallows them; Instagram cannot be interacted with
         behind the lock screen.
      6. Tap **Request partner code** → the lock screen steps aside, WhatsApp or SMS opens, and the
         partner sends the code back. Return to Instagram to enter it.
      7. Enter a wrong code → *"That code is not valid."* and the entry is wiped. The block stays up
         until the code expires.
      8. Enter the correct code → the overlay closes, Instagram becomes usable for 5 minutes and the
         status card shows a *"PAUSED"* countdown.
      9. Wait 5 minutes → the block is re-armed automatically; opening Instagram brings the lock
         screen back.
      10. From the active dashboard, **Pause for 15 minutes** (partner code required) grants a longer
          break. The dashboard shows the countdown and **Resume blocking** ends it immediately. Usage
          keeps counting during the pause, so the block returns if the allowance is still exhausted.
      11. **Reset today's usage** clears the counter (partner code required), **Turn the blocker off**
          disables the limiter entirely (partner code required).
      
      **No Instagram installed?** Arm a rule, then use the *Preview the lock screen* button on the
      active dashboard — it raises exactly the same overlay over ShayLock itself.
      
      ## 6. How it works
      
      ```
      com.example.shaylock
      ├── ShayLockApp.kt                      Application: initialises state + schedules health work
      ├── MainActivity.kt                     Compose setup/dashboard + partner approval flow
      ├── core/
      │   ├── AccountabilityController.kt     One-time codes, partner storage, WhatsApp/SMS deep links
      │   ├── BlockPolicy.kt                  Blocked packages, allowances, session and pause durations
      │   ├── DailyLimit.kt                   The daily allowance + today's usage (wall-clock based)
      │   ├── BlockController.kt              Single source of truth + SharedPreferences persistence
      │   ├── PermissionUtils.kt              Accessibility / overlay / install-state checks
      │   └── TimeFormat.kt                   Duration, allowance and countdown formatting
      ├── service/
      │   ├── BlockerAccessibilityService.kt  The watchdog (TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED + key filter)
      │   ├── OverlayService.kt               Foreground service that owns the lock window
      │   ├── OverlayWindowHost.kt            The actual TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY dialog window
      │   ├── OverlayLifecycleOwner.kt        Lifecycle owner for the Compose view in the dialog
      │   ├── OverlayController.kt            Facade used by the watchdog and the UI
      │   └── ServiceHealthWorker.kt          WorkManager check that the watchdog is still enabled
      └── ui/
          ├── screens/SetupScreen.kt          Progressive permissions + partner + dashboard
          ├── screens/OverlayScreen.kt        The lock screen with the partner-code keypad
          ├── screens/ApprovalDialog.kt       One-time-code dialog for protected settings
          ├── components/CodeKeypad.kt        Shared numeric keypad + code dots
          └── theme/                          Dark-only ink palette, type scale and shapes
      ```
      
      * **Daily allowance** — `DailyLimit` holds the fixed allowance, the time consumed today and the
        day the usage belongs to. `rolledToDay()` resets only the usage counter at local midnight; the
        allowance itself never drifts.
      * **Watchdog** — `BlockerAccessibilityService` listens for `TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED`. It reports
        every foreground change to `BlockController`, which charges the allowance only while a blocked
        app is really on screen (the counter pauses when the user leaves the app and when the lock
        screen covers it). It also swallows `HOME`, `BACK` and `APP_SWITCH` key events while the
        overlay is visible (`flagRequestFilterKeyEvents` + `canRequestFilterKeyEvents` in
        `res/xml/accessibility_service_config.xml`).
      * **Overlay** — `OverlayWindowHost` creates a `Dialog` with `TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY`,
        `setCancelable(false)`, `setCanceledOnTouchOutside(false)` and a key listener that eats Back.
        A `Dialog` window is modal, so touches never reach Instagram behind it.
      * **State** — `BlockController` holds the allowance, today's usage and the unlock session,
        persists all of them to `SharedPreferences` (so they survive process death) and exposes them as
        `StateFlow`s that the UI collects. A 1-second ticker keeps the counters accurate, rolls the day
        over at midnight and re-arms the block the moment a session expires.
      * **Accountability** — `AccountabilityController` creates a four-digit code with a five-minute
        lifetime, keeps it in memory only, and opens `https://wa.me/<partner>?text=…` (WhatsApp) or an
        `smsto:` intent (SMS) with a pre-filled message. `verifyAndConsume()` checks the purpose and
        expiry and invalidates the code on success.
      * **Approval gate** — `BlockController.applyDailyLimit()`, `setLimiterEnabled()`,
        `resetTodayUsage()`, `pauseFor()` and `unlockWithCode()` all consume a code for their specific
        purpose and refuse to act without it. The single exception is the first setup: while no
        allowance has ever been configured, the limit can be armed without approval so the user can get
        started.
      * **Partner changes** — the first partner is saved directly; replacing an existing partner
        consumes a `CHANGE_PARTNER` code, so the user cannot redirect approvals to a number they
        control after setup.
      * **Resilience** — the overlay service returns `START_STICKY`, reinitialises the persisted
        controllers after a process restart and asks the watchdog to re-evaluate the current window.
        `ServiceHealthWorker` runs every 15 minutes through WorkManager; if a rule is enabled but the
        accessibility service is off in system settings, it posts a high-priority notification that
        opens the accessibility settings screen. The warning is cleared once protection is healthy
        again.
      
      ## 7. Prototype limitations
      
      * **The code is not a cryptographic secret.** The WhatsApp/SMS message is composed locally and
        pre-filled with the code, so a determined device owner can read it before sending. This is an
        accountability boundary, not a security boundary. A real product needs a backend that sends
        the code to the partner only after they approve.
      * The partner phone number is stored locally and is not verified. It must include a country code.
      * Only one accessibility service can hold the key-filtering capability at a time. If another
        key-filtering service is enabled, HOME/BACK may leak through; the overlay still re-arms
        whenever Instagram comes back to the foreground.
      * On gesture-navigation devices the HOME key is usually not delivered to accessibility
        services at all, so the HOME gesture is not swallowed — the overlay window itself stays on
        top of the launcher.
      * The usage counter uses wall-clock time, so changing the device clock shifts it. The allowance
        itself is unaffected.
      * Usage is only counted while the accessibility watchdog is running. The WorkManager health check
        warns about a disabled service, but Android does not allow the app to re-enable it silently.
      * The allowance is charged from the moment the limiter is switched on, so enabling it late in
        the day still gives the full allowance from that point on.
      * WorkManager's minimum periodic interval is 15 minutes. A disabled accessibility service may
        therefore go unnoticed for up to ~15 minutes; Instagram itself remains blocked only when the
        watchdog is actually running.
      * Only Instagram (`com.instagram.android`) and Instagram Lite (`com.instagram.lite`) are
        blocked. Add more package ids to `BlockPolicy.BLOCKED_PACKAGES` to extend it.
      
- path: UI_POLISH_PLAN.md
  content: |
      # ShayLock UI Polish Plan
      
      ## Overview
      Polish ShayLock to look like a professional, high-end production app using Jetpack Compose and Material 3.
      
      ## Requirements
      1. Setup/Dashboard Screen - Material 3 cards with soft tonal containers, status badges, clean duration formatting, live countdown banner
      2. Full-Screen Lock Overlay - Dark theme #0B0B0F, shield icon, 4-dot PIN indicator, custom numeric keypad, error handling
      3. Typography & Micro-interactions - Material 3 hierarchy, smooth transitions, clean spacing
      
      ## Plan
      - Enhance theme (colors, typography)
      - Update TimeFormat for cleaner durations
      - Polish PinKeypad component
      - Polish OverlayScreen
      - Polish SetupScreen with better cards/badges
      - Polish PinDialog

## User

Act as an expert Android Developer using Kotlin, Jetpack Compose, and modern Android architecture. 

I want to build a strict screen-time blocking app targeting Android. 

### Core Goal:
Prevent the user from opening the Instagram app (`com.instagram.android`) once a set timer/block rule is active. Unlike standard screen time limiters, there should be NO option to easily extend time or bypass the block, except by entering a hardcoded 4-digit PIN.

### Requirements & Functionality:
1. Accessibility Service (The Blocker Watchdog):
   - Create a custom `AccessibilityService` that monitors foreground app events (`TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED`).

======== session-ses_ea18.md ========
## User

Act as an expert Android Developer using Kotlin, Jetpack Compose, and modern Android architecture. 

I want to build a strict screen-time blocking app targeting Android. 

### Core Goal:
Prevent the user from opening the Instagram app (`com.instagram.android`) once a set timer/block rule is active. Unlike standard screen time limiters, there should be NO option to easily extend time or bypass the block, except by entering a hardcoded 4-digit PIN.

### Requirements & Functionality:
1. Accessibility Service (The Blocker Watchdog):
   - Create a custom `AccessibilityService` that monitors foreground app events (`TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED`).
   - Detect when `com.instagram.android` is brought to the foreground.
   - If Instagram is blocked, immediately trigger a full-screen lock overlay over Instagram.

2. Full-Screen Blocking Overlay (`SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`):
   - Display a full-screen blocking overlay over Instagram.
   - Intercept/disable the back gesture and home actions while the overlay is visible to prevent easy bypass.
   - Show a clear message: "Instagram is Blocked".
   - Include a 4-digit PIN input field and an "Unlock" button.

3. Hardcoded PIN & Session Timer Logic:
   - For this prototype, hardcode the PIN to "1234".
   - When the correct PIN ("1234") is entered:
     - Dismiss the blocking overlay.
     - Grant a temporary 5-minute unlock session using a background timer.
     - Once the 5-minute timer expires, automatically re-enable strict blocking for Instagram.
   - If an incorrect PIN is entered, show an error message and keep Instagram blocked.

4. Permissions & Setup UI:
   - Create a clean Jetpack Compose setup screen.
   - Include clear status toggles/buttons directing the user to grant necessary system permissions:
     a) Accessibility Service permission
     b) "Display over other apps" (`SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`) permission

### Expected Deliverables:
- Complete Kotlin code structure (`MainActivity.kt`, `BlockerAccessibilityService.kt`, `OverlayService.kt` / Compose UI for Overlay).
- `AndroidManifest.xml` setup including all required permissions, accessibility config XML, and service declarations.
- Concise step-by-step instructions on how to set up the project and test it on a physical Android device.

Please write clean, well-commented Kotlin code ready to run in Android Studio.

- path: app\src\main\java\com\example\shaylock\core\BlockPolicy.kt
  content: |
      package com.example.shaylock.core
      
      /**
       * Single source of truth for the hard-coded blocking policy of this prototype.
       *
       * Everything the blocker knows about "what to block" and "how to unlock" lives here so the
       * accessibility watchdog, the overlay service and the setup UI can never drift apart.
       */
      object BlockPolicy {
      
          /**
           * The hard-coded PIN that lifts the block and protects the app settings.
           *
           * PROTOTYPE LIMITATION: this value is compiled into the APK and can be recovered by anyone
           * who decompiles it. For a real product store a salted hash in the Android Keystore instead.
           */
          const val PIN: String = "2310"
      
          /** Number of digits the PIN must contain. */
          const val PIN_LENGTH: Int = 4
      
          /**
           * Pure check of a PIN entry.
           *
           * Kept free of any side effect so it can be unit tested: nothing here grants a session or
           * touches the persisted state.
           */
          fun isCorrectPin(code: String): Boolean =
              code.length == PIN_LENGTH && code == PIN
      
          /**
           * Packages that are blocked when a rule is active.
           *
           * [PRIMARY_PACKAGE] is Instagram proper; Instagram Lite is included because it is a
           * separate app id that would otherwise be an obvious bypass.
           */
          val BLOCKED_PACKAGES: Set<String> = setOf(
              "com.instagram.android",
              "com.instagram.lite",
          )
      
          /** The app the overlay is built around; used for the title shown on the lock screen. */
          const val PRIMARY_PACKAGE: String = "com.instagram.android"
      
          /**
           * How long a correct PIN keeps the blocked app accessible.
           *
           * Once the window elapses the watchdog re-arms the block automatically.
           */
          const val UNLOCK_SESSION_MS: Long = 5 * 60 * 1000L
      
          /**
           * How long the "Pause" action suspends the block.
           *
           * The pause is deliberately short and PIN-gated: it is an escape hatch, not a way to disable
           * protection for the rest of the day.
           */
          const val PAUSE_DURATION_MS: Long = 15 * 60 * 1000L
      
          /** Convenience predicate used by the watchdog. */
          fun isBlockedPackage(packageName: String?): Boolean =
              packageName != null && BLOCKED_PACKAGES.contains(packageName)
      
          /**
           * Selectable daily allowances offered in the setup UI, in milliseconds.
           *
           * The chosen value is fixed: it stays exactly as selected until it is changed again with the
           * PIN. Only the usage counter is reset, and that happens automatically at local midnight.
           */
          val DAILY_LIMIT_OPTIONS: List<Long> = listOf(
              10 * 1000L, // Test option: the allowance runs out after 10 seconds.
              15 * 60 * 1000L,
              30 * 60 * 1000L,
              60 * 60 * 1000L,
              2 * 60 * 60 * 1000L,
              3 * 60 * 60 * 1000L,
              5 * 60 * 60 * 1000L,
          )
      
          /** The allowance that is offered by default when nothing is configured yet. */
          val DEFAULT_DAILY_LIMIT_MS: Long = 30 * 60 * 1000L
      }
      
- path: app\src\main\java\com\example\shaylock\MainActivity.kt

      package com.example.shaylock
      
      import android.Manifest

      /**
       * The single activity of the app: it hosts the setup / dashboard screen.
       *
       * Everything else runs in the background:
       *  - [com.example.shaylock.service.BlockerAccessibilityService] watches the foreground app and
       *    measures how much of the daily allowance is actually consumed.
       *  - [com.example.shaylock.service.OverlayService] draws the strict lock screen.
       */
      class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
      
          private var permissionState by mutableStateOf(PermissionUiState())
          private var selectedLimitMs by mutableLongStateOf(BlockPolicy.DEFAULT_DAILY_LIMIT_MS)
          private var pinRequest by mutableStateOf<PinRequest?>(null)
      
          private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
          private val permissionRecheck = Runnable { refreshPermissionState() }
      
          private val notificationPermissionLauncher =
              registerForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()) { granted ->
                  refreshPermissionState()
                  if (!granted) {
                      // Not fatal: the foreground service still runs, only the notification is hidden.
                      android.util.Log.i(TAG, "Notifications denied - the blocker still works.")
                  }
              }
      
          override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
              super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
              enableEdgeToEdge(
                  statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(android.graphics.Color.TRANSPARENT),
                  navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(android.graphics.Color.TRANSPARENT),
              )
      
              BlockController.initialize(applicationContext)
              selectedLimitMs = BlockController.dailyLimit.value.limitMs
                  .takeIf { it > 0L }
                  ?: BlockPolicy.DEFAULT_DAILY_LIMIT_MS
              refreshPermissionState()
      
              setContent {
                  ShayLockTheme {
                      val limit by BlockController.dailyLimit.collectAsState()
                      val usedTodayMs by BlockController.usedTodayMs.collectAsState()
                      val limitRemainingMs by BlockController.limitRemainingMs.collectAsState()
                      val isBlockingEffective by BlockController.isBlockingEffective.collectAsState()
                      val sessionRemainingMs by BlockController.sessionRemainingMs.collectAsState()
      
                      Box(
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .fillMaxSize()
                              .background(Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(InkTop, InkBottom))),
                      ) {
                          Scaffold(
                              modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                              containerColor = Color.Transparent,
                          ) { innerPadding ->
                              SetupScreen(
                                  modifier = Modifier.padding(innerPadding),
                                  permissionState = permissionState,
                                  limit = limit,
                                  usedTodayMs = usedTodayMs,
                                  limitRemainingMs = limitRemainingMs,
                                  isBlockingEffective = isBlockingEffective,
                                  sessionRemainingMs = sessionRemainingMs,
                                  selectedLimitMs = selectedLimitMs,
                                  onSelectLimit = { selectedLimitMs = it },
                                  onRequestSetLimit = { limitMs -> requestSetLimit(limitMs) },
                                  onRequestDisable = { pinRequest = PinRequest.Disable },
                                  onRequestResetUsage = {
                                      pinRequest = PinRequest.ResetUsage
                                  },
                                  onRequestPause = {
                                      pinRequest = PinRequest.Pause(
                                          BlockPolicy.PAUSE_DURATION_MS,
                                      )
                                  },
                                  onResumeNow = { BlockController.endSession() },
                                  onOpenAccessibilitySettings = { openAccessibilitySettings() },
                                  onOpenOverlaySettings = { openOverlaySettings() },
                                  onRequestNotifications = { requestNotificationPermission() },
                                  onPreviewOverlay = { previewOverlay() },
                              )
                          }
      
                          val request = pinRequest
                          if (request != null) {
                              PinDialog(
                                  title = request.title,
                                  message = request.message,
                                  confirmLabel = "Confirm",
                                  onDismiss = { pinRequest = null },
                                  onConfirm = { pin -> confirmWithPin(request, pin) },
                              )
                          }
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      
          override fun onResume() {
              super.onResume()
              // Both permissions are granted on separate system screens, so refresh on every resume.
              refreshPermissionState()
              schedulePermissionRechecks()
          }
      
          override fun onPause() {
              mainHandler.removeCallbacks(permissionRecheck)
              super.onPause()
          }
      
          override fun onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus: Boolean) {
              super.onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus)
              if (hasFocus) refreshPermissionState()
          }
      
          private fun schedulePermissionRechecks() {
              mainHandler.removeCallbacks(permissionRecheck)
              listOf(300L, 1_200L, 3_000L).forEach { delay ->
                  mainHandler.postDelayed(permissionRecheck, delay)
              }
          }
      
          /**
           * Sets the first daily allowance without a PIN; later changes go through [PinRequest.SetLimit].
           */
          private fun requestSetLimit(limitMs: Long) {
              if (BlockController.isPinRequiredToSetLimit()) {
                  pinRequest = PinRequest.SetLimit(limitMs)
                  return
              }
      
              val accepted = BlockController.applyDailyLimit(limitMs, pin = null)
              android.util.Log.i(TAG, "Initial limit=$limitMs accepted=$accepted")
              if (accepted) {
                  OverlayController.keepAlive(this)
              }
          }
      
          /**
           * Runs the protected action after the PIN was entered.
           *
           * @return true when the PIN was correct, which lets the dialog close.
           */
          private fun confirmWithPin(request: PinRequest, code: String): Boolean {
              val accepted = when (request) {
                  is PinRequest.SetLimit -> BlockController.applyDailyLimit(request.limitMs, code)
                  is PinRequest.Disable -> BlockController.setLimiterEnabled(false, code)
                  is PinRequest.ResetUsage -> BlockController.resetTodayUsage(code)
                  is PinRequest.Pause -> BlockController.pauseFor(request.durationMs, code)
              }
              android.util.Log.i(TAG, "PIN request=$request accepted=$accepted")
      
              if (accepted) {
                  when (request) {
                      is PinRequest.SetLimit -> OverlayController.keepAlive(this)
                      is PinRequest.Disable -> OverlayController.stop(this)
                      is PinRequest.ResetUsage -> Unit
                      is PinRequest.Pause -> Unit
                  }
              }
              return accepted
          }
      
          private fun openAccessibilitySettings() {
              launchFirstAvailable(
                  Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS),
                  Intent(Settings.ACTION_SETTINGS),
              )
          }
      
          private fun openOverlaySettings() {
              val packageUri = "package:$packageName".toUri()
              launchFirstAvailable(
                  Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION, packageUri),
                  Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_OVERLAY_PERMISSION),
                  Intent(Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS, packageUri),
              )
          }
      
          /**
           * Opens the first settings screen the device actually supports. Some OEMs ship only one of
           * these intents, or reject the package-specific variant.
           */
          private fun launchFirstAvailable(vararg intents: Intent) {
              for (intent in intents) {
                  if (runCatching { startActivity(intent) }.isSuccess) return
              }
              android.util.Log.w(TAG, "No matching system settings screen is available")
          }
      
          private fun requestNotificationPermission() {
              if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                  notificationPermissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
              } else {
                  refreshPermissionState()
              }
          }
      
          /** Raises the lock screen over this app so the flow can be verified without Instagram. */
          private fun previewOverlay() {
              if (!PermissionUtils.canDrawOverlays(this)) {
                  openOverlaySettings()
                  return
              }
              OverlayController.show(this, BlockPolicy.PRIMARY_PACKAGE)
          }
      
          private fun refreshPermissionState() {
              val accessibilityToggleOn = PermissionUtils.isAccessibilityServiceEnabledInSettings(this)
              permissionState = PermissionUiState(
                  accessibilityEnabled = accessibilityToggleOn &&
                      PermissionUtils.isAccessibilityServiceRunning(),
                  accessibilityToggleOn = accessibilityToggleOn,
                  overlayGranted = PermissionUtils.canDrawOverlays(this),
                  notificationsGranted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                      checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) ==
                          PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
                  } else {
                      true
                  },
                  targetInstalled = PermissionUtils.isTargetInstalled(this),
              )
          }
      
          private companion object {
              const val TAG = "MainActivity"
          }
      }
      
      /**
       * The protected actions that need the PIN before they are applied.
       */
      private sealed interface PinRequest {
      
          val title: String
          val message: String
      
          data class SetLimit(val limitMs: Long) : PinRequest {
              override val title: String = "Enter your PIN"
              override val message: String =
                  "Confirm the daily limit of ${formatLimit(limitMs)}."
          }
      
          data object Disable : PinRequest {
              override val title: String = "Enter your PIN"
              override val message: String =
                  "Turning the blocker off needs your PIN."
          }
      
          data object ResetUsage : PinRequest {
              override val title: String = "Enter your PIN"
              override val message: String =
                  "Clearing today's usage needs your PIN."
          }
      
          data class Pause(val durationMs: Long) : PinRequest {
              override val title: String = "Enter your PIN"
              override val message: String =
                  "Pausing for ${durationMs / 60_000} minutes needs your PIN."
          }
      }

      /**
       * The single dashboard / onboarding screen.
       *
       * The screen reveals itself progressively:
       *  1. only missing required permissions are shown,
       *  2. once they are granted the permission section collapses and the allowance picker appears,
       *  3. once an allowance is armed the picker gives way to the focused active dashboard.
       */
      @Composable
      fun SetupScreen(
          modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
          permissionState: PermissionUiState,
          limit: DailyLimit,
          usedTodayMs: Long,
          limitRemainingMs: Long,
          isBlockingEffective: Boolean,
          sessionRemainingMs: Long,
          selectedLimitMs: Long,
          onSelectLimit: (Long) -> Unit,
          onRequestSetLimit: (Long) -> Unit,
          onRequestDisable: () -> Unit,
          onRequestResetUsage: () -> Unit,
          onRequestPause: () -> Unit,
          onResumeNow: () -> Unit,
          onOpenAccessibilitySettings: () -> Unit,
          onOpenOverlaySettings: () -> Unit,
          onRequestNotifications: () -> Unit,
          onPreviewOverlay: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          val ready = permissionState.readyToBlock
          val isDashboard = ready && limit.isEnabled
          val showPermissions = !ready
          val showAllowanceSetup = ready && !limit.isEnabled
          val showDashboard = isDashboard
      
          var showLimitEditor by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
          LaunchedEffect(limit.limitMs, limit.isEnabled) {
              if (limit.isEnabled) showLimitEditor = false
          }
      
          Column(
              modifier = modifier
                  .fillMaxSize()
                  .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                  .padding(start = 20.dp, end = 20.dp, top = 20.dp, bottom = 40.dp),
          ) {
              ScreenHeader(
                  isActive = isDashboard,
                  isPaused = sessionRemainingMs > 0L,
              )
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(26.dp))
      
              StatusHero(
                  permissionState = permissionState,
                  limit = limit,
                  usedTodayMs = usedTodayMs,
                  limitRemainingMs = limitRemainingMs,
                  isBlockingEffective = isBlockingEffective,
                  sessionRemainingMs = sessionRemainingMs,
              )
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
              InlineNotice(
                  icon = Icons.Filled.Info,
                  text = "Unlocking, pausing and settings changes need PIN 2310.",
                  color = InkAccent,
                  container = InkAccentContainer,
              )
      
              if (!permissionState.targetInstalled) {
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                  InlineNotice(
                      icon = Icons.Filled.Warning,
                      text = "Instagram does not appear to be installed, so there is nothing to " +
                          "block yet.",
                      color = InkWarning,
                      container = InkWarningContainer,
                  )
              }
      
              /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
               * Permissions - only while something is missing
               * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
              AnimatedVisibility(
                  visible = showPermissions,
                  enter = fadeIn(tween(260)) + expandVertically(tween(320)),
                  exit = fadeOut(tween(180)) + shrinkVertically(tween(280)),
              ) {
                  Column {
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(30.dp))
      
                      SectionLabel("GET PROTECTED")
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
      
                      if (!permissionState.accessibilityEnabled) {
                          PermissionRow(
                              icon = Icons.Filled.Settings,
                              title = "Accessibility service",
                              description = "Lets ShayLock notice when Instagram is on screen and " +
                                  "measure the time it is actually used.",
                              status = if (permissionState.accessibilityConnecting) {
                                  PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING
                              } else {
                                  PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED
                              },
                              steps = listOf(
                                  "Open Settings → Accessibility.",
                                  "Tap ShayLock under Installed services.",
                                  "Turn on \"Use service\" and confirm the prompt.",
                              ),
                              actionLabel = "Open settings",
                              onAction = onOpenAccessibilitySettings,
                          )
      
                          if (!permissionState.overlayGranted ||
                              !permissionState.notificationsGranted
                          ) {
                              Hairline()
                          }
                      }
      
                      if (!permissionState.overlayGranted) {
                          PermissionRow(
                              icon = Icons.Filled.Warning,
                              title = "Display over other apps",
                              description = "Allows the full-screen lock screen to cover Instagram.",
                              status = PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED,
                              steps = listOf(
                                  "Open Settings → Apps → ShayLock.",
                                  "Tap \"Display over other apps\".",
                                  "Turn the switch on.",
                              ),
                              actionLabel = "Open settings",
                              onAction = onOpenOverlaySettings,
                          )
      
                          if (!permissionState.notificationsGranted) {
                              Hairline()
                          }
                      }
      
                      if (!permissionState.notificationsGranted) {
                          PermissionRow(
                              icon = Icons.Filled.Notifications,
                              title = "Notifications",
                              description = "Optional. Shows the ongoing protection notification.",
                              status = PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL,
                              steps = listOf("Tap \"Allow\" on the Android prompt."),
                              actionLabel = "Allow",
                              onAction = onRequestNotifications,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
      
              /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
               * Allowance setup - once both permissions are active
               * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
              AnimatedVisibility(
                  visible = showAllowanceSetup,
                  enter = fadeIn(tween(260)) + expandVertically(tween(320)),
                  exit = fadeOut(tween(180)) + shrinkVertically(tween(280)),
              ) {
                  Column {
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(30.dp))
      
                      SectionLabel("DAILY ALLOWANCE")
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
      
                      Text(
                          text = "Choose how much Instagram is allowed each day.",
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                          color = InkTextPrimary,
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = "Only time Instagram is really on screen counts.",
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                          color = InkTextSecondary,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
      
                      AllowanceChips(
                          selected = selectedLimitMs,
                          onSelect = onSelectLimit,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
      
                      Button(
                          onClick = { onRequestSetLimit(selectedLimitMs) },
