```
                              .fillMaxWidth()
                              .height(54.dp),
                          shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                          colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                              containerColor = InkAccent,
                              contentColor = InkOnAccent,
                          ),
                      ) {
                          Icon(
                              painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_lock_shield),
                              contentDescription = null,
                              modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp),
                          )
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                          Text("Start blocking", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                      }
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = "The first setup needs no PIN. Every later change needs the PIN.",
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                          color = InkTextTertiary,
                      )
                  }
              }
      
              /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
               * Active dashboard
               * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
              AnimatedVisibility(
                  visible = showDashboard,
                  enter = fadeIn(tween(260)) + expandVertically(tween(320)),
                  exit = fadeOut(tween(180)) + shrinkVertically(tween(280)),
              ) {
                  Column {
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(30.dp))
      
                      SectionLabel("CONTROLS")
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
      
                      when {
                          sessionRemainingMs > 0L -> {
                              Button(
                                  onClick = onResumeNow,
                                  modifier = Modifier
                                      .fillMaxWidth()
                                      .height(52.dp),
                                  shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                                  colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                      containerColor = InkAccent,
                                      contentColor = InkOnAccent,
                                  ),
                              ) {
                                  Text("Resume blocking", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                              }
                          }
      
                          isBlockingEffective -> {
                              Button(
                                  onClick = onRequestPause,
                                  modifier = Modifier
                                      .fillMaxWidth()
                                      .height(52.dp),
                                  shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                                  colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                      containerColor = InkWarningContainer,
                                      contentColor = InkOnWarningContainer,
                                  ),
                              ) {
                                  Icon(
                                      imageVector = Icons.Filled.Warning,
                                      contentDescription = null,
                                      modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp),
                                  )
                                  Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                  Text("Pause for 15 minutes", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                              }
                          }
                      }
      
                      if (sessionRemainingMs > 0L || isBlockingEffective) {
                          Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                      }
      
                      ActionRow(
                          title = "Daily allowance",
                          subtitle = "Currently ${formatLimit(limit.limitMs)} per day",
                          action = if (showLimitEditor) "Close" else "Change",
                          onClick = { showLimitEditor = !showLimitEditor },
                      )
      
                      AnimatedVisibility(
                          visible = showLimitEditor,
                          enter = fadeIn(tween(200)) + expandVertically(tween(260)),
                          exit = fadeOut(tween(160)) + shrinkVertically(tween(240)),
                      ) {
                          Column {
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                              AllowanceChips(
                                  selected = selectedLimitMs,
                                  onSelect = onSelectLimit,
                              )
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                              Button(
                                  onClick = { onRequestSetLimit(selectedLimitMs) },
                                  modifier = Modifier
                                      .fillMaxWidth()
                                      .height(48.dp),
                                  shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                                  colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                      containerColor = InkAccentContainer,
                                      contentColor = InkAccent,
                                  ),
                              ) {
                                  Text("Save new limit", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                              }
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                              Text(
                                  text = "Saving a new limit needs your PIN.",
                                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                  color = InkTextTertiary,
                              )
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                          }
                      }
      
                      Hairline()
      
                      ActionRow(
                          title = "Reset today's usage",
                          subtitle = "${formatLimit(usedTodayMs)} used today",
                          action = "Reset",
                          onClick = onRequestResetUsage,
                      )
      
                      Hairline()
      
                      ActionRow(
                          title = "Turn off the blocker",
                          subtitle = "Removes the rule until you set it again",
                          action = "Turn off",
                          destructive = true,
                          onClick = onRequestDisable,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
      
                      TextButton(
                          onClick = onPreviewOverlay,
                          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                      ) {
                          Icon(
                              painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_lock_shield),
                              contentDescription = null,
                              modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp),
                              tint = InkTextSecondary,
                          )
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                          Text(
                              text = "Preview the lock screen",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                              color = InkTextSecondary,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
      
              if (!ready) {
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = "The Start blocking button appears once both required permissions " +
                          "are active.",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextTertiary,
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(34.dp))
      
              Footer(
                  ready = ready,
                  notificationsGranted = permissionState.notificationsGranted,
                  onRequestNotifications = onRequestNotifications,
              )
      
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Header and hero
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun ScreenHeader(isActive: Boolean, isPaused: Boolean) {
          Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
              Box(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .size(42.dp)
                      .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                      .background(InkAccentContainer),
                  contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
              ) {
                  Icon(
                      painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_lock_shield),
                      contentDescription = null,
                      tint = InkAccent,
                      modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp),
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
              Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                  Text(
                      text = "ShayLock",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                      color = InkTextPrimary,
                  )
                  Text(
                      text = "Strict screen-time blocker",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                      color = InkTextTertiary,
                      letterSpacing = 0.4.sp,
                  )
              }
      
              val label: String
              val container: Color
              val content: Color
              when {
                  isPaused -> {
                      label = "PAUSED"
                      container = InkWarningContainer
                      content = InkOnWarningContainer
                  }
      
                  isActive -> {
                      label = "ACTIVE"
                      container = InkSuccessContainer
                      content = InkSuccess
                  }
      
                  else -> {
                      label = "SETUP"
                      container = InkSurfaceHigh
                      content = InkTextSecondary
                  }
              }
      
              Row(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .clip(CircleShape)
                      .background(container)
                      .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp),
                  verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
              ) {
                  Box(
                      modifier = Modifier
                          .size(5.dp)
                          .clip(CircleShape)
                          .background(content),
                  )
                  Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = label,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                      color = content,
                  )
              }
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun StatusHero(
          permissionState: PermissionUiState,
          limit: DailyLimit,
          usedTodayMs: Long,
          limitRemainingMs: Long,
          isBlockingEffective: Boolean,
          sessionRemainingMs: Long,
      ) {
          val accent = when {
              !permissionState.readyToBlock &&
                  permissionState.accessibilityConnecting -> InkWarning
              !permissionState.readyToBlock -> InkDanger
              sessionRemainingMs > 0L -> InkWarning
              isBlockingEffective -> InkDanger
              limit.isEnabled -> InkSuccess
              else -> InkAccent
          }
          val animatedAccent by animateColorAsState(
              targetValue = accent,
              animationSpec = tween(400),
              label = "heroAccent",
          )
      
          val eyebrow: String
          val value: String
          val suffix: String
          val detail: String
          when {
              !permissionState.readyToBlock && permissionState.accessibilityConnecting -> {
                  eyebrow = "CONNECTING"
                  value = "Almost there"
                  suffix = ""
                  detail = "Android is finishing the accessibility connection."
              }
      
              !permissionState.readyToBlock -> {
                  eyebrow = "NOT PROTECTED"
                  value = "Setup required"
                  suffix = ""
                  detail = "Grant the missing permissions below to start protecting Instagram."
              }
      
              sessionRemainingMs > 0L -> {
                  eyebrow = "PAUSED"
                  value = formatCountdown(sessionRemainingMs)
                  suffix = "left"
                  detail = "Blocking resumes automatically when the pause ends."
              }
      
              isBlockingEffective -> {
                  eyebrow = "BLOCKED"
                  value = "Locked"
                  suffix = "until midnight"
                  detail = "Today's ${formatLimit(limit.limitMs)} allowance is used up."
              }
      
              limit.isEnabled -> {
                  eyebrow = "PROTECTED"
                  value = formatLimit(limitRemainingMs)
                  suffix = "left today"
                  detail = "Only time Instagram is really on screen counts."
              }
      
              else -> {
                  eyebrow = "READY"
                  value = "No limit yet"
                  suffix = ""
                  detail = "Choose a daily allowance below to arm the blocker."
              }
          }
      
          Box(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(26.dp))
                  .background(InkSurface),
          ) {
              Box(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .matchParentSize()
                      .background(
                          Brush.verticalGradient(
                              colors = listOf(
                                  animatedAccent.copy(alpha = 0.08f),
                                  Color.Transparent,
                              ),
                          ),
                      ),
              )
      
              Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(22.dp)) {
                  Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                      Box(
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .size(5.dp)
                              .clip(CircleShape)
                              .background(animatedAccent),
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(7.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = eyebrow,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                          color = animatedAccent,
                          letterSpacing = 1.4.sp,
                      )
                  }
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
      
                  Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom) {
                      Text(
                          text = value,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.displaySmall,
                          color = InkTextPrimary,
                      )
                      if (suffix.isNotEmpty()) {
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(9.dp))
                          Text(
                              text = suffix,
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                              color = InkTextSecondary,
                              modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 5.dp),
                          )
                      }
                  }
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
      
                  Text(
                      text = detail,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
      
                  if (limit.isEnabled && limit.limitMs > 0L) {
                      val progress by animateFloatAsState(
                          targetValue = limit.usedFraction(usedTodayMs),
                          animationSpec = tween(600),
                          label = "usageProgress",
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
      
                      LinearProgressIndicator(
                          progress = { progress },
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .fillMaxWidth()
                              .height(4.dp)
                              .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50)),
                          color = animatedAccent,
                          trackColor = InkHairline,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
      
                      Row {
                          Text(
                              text = "${formatLimit(usedTodayMs)} used",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                              color = InkTextTertiary,
                              letterSpacing = 0.3.sp,
                              modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                          )
                          Text(
                              text = "${formatLimit(limit.limitMs)} per day",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                              color = InkTextTertiary,
                              letterSpacing = 0.3.sp,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Permission rows
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      private enum class PermissionRowStatus(val label: String) {
          CONNECTING("Connecting…"),
          REQUIRED("Action required"),
          OPTIONAL("Optional"),
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun PermissionRow(
          icon: ImageVector,
          title: String,
          description: String,
          status: PermissionRowStatus,
          steps: List<String>,
          actionLabel: String,
          onAction: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          val statusColor = when (status) {
              PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING -> InkWarning
              PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED -> InkDanger
              PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL -> InkTextTertiary
          }
          val iconContainer = when (status) {
              PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING -> InkWarningContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED -> InkDangerContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL -> InkSurfaceHigh
          }
          val iconContent = when (status) {
              PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING -> InkOnWarningContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED -> InkOnDangerContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL -> InkTextSecondary
          }
      
          Column(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .padding(vertical = 16.dp),
          ) {
              Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                  Box(
                      modifier = Modifier
                          .size(38.dp)
                          .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                          .background(iconContainer),
                      contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                  ) {
                      Icon(
                          imageVector = icon,
                          contentDescription = null,
                          tint = iconContent,
                          modifier = Modifier.size(19.dp),
                      )
                  }
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
                  Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                      Text(
                          text = title,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                          color = InkTextPrimary,
                      )
                      Text(
                          text = status.label,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                          color = statusColor,
                          letterSpacing = 0.5.sp,
                      )
                  }
      
                  TextButton(
                      onClick = onAction,
                      colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = InkAccent),
                  ) {
                      Text(actionLabel, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                  }
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = description,
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                  color = InkTextSecondary,
                  modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 50.dp),
              )
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
              Column(
                  modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 50.dp),
                  verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp),
              ) {
                  steps.forEachIndexed { index, step ->
                      Row {
                          Text(
                              text = "${index + 1}.",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                              color = statusColor,
                          )
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                          Text(
                              text = step,
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                              color = InkTextTertiary,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Allowance editor
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun AllowanceChips(selected: Long, onSelect: (Long) -> Unit) {
          val options = BlockPolicy.DAILY_LIMIT_OPTIONS
      
          Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
              options.chunked(3).forEach { row ->
                  Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                      row.forEach { option ->
                          val isSelected = selected == option
                          Row(
                              modifier = Modifier
                                  .weight(1f)
                                  .height(46.dp)
                                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                                  .background(if (isSelected) InkAccentContainer else InkSurface)
                                  .clickable(role = Role.RadioButton) { onSelect(option) }
                                  .padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                              horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                          ) {
                              if (isSelected) {
                                  Icon(
                                      imageVector = Icons.Filled.Check,
                                      contentDescription = null,
                                      tint = InkAccent,
                                      modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp),
                                  )
                                  Spacer(Modifier.width(5.dp))
                              }
                              Text(
                                  text = formatLimit(option),
                                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                                  color = if (isSelected) InkAccent else InkTextSecondary,
                              )
                          }
                      }
                      repeat(3 - row.size) {
                          Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Dashboard action rows and footer
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun ActionRow(
          title: String,
          subtitle: String,
          action: String,
          onClick: () -> Unit,
          destructive: Boolean = false,
      ) {
          Row(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clickable(role = Role.Button, onClick = onClick)
                  .padding(vertical = 17.dp),
              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
          ) {
              Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                  Text(
                      text = title,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                      color = if (destructive) InkDanger else InkTextPrimary,
                  )
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = subtitle,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = action,
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                  color = if (destructive) InkDanger else InkAccent,
              )
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun PartnerRow(
          partnerPhone: String?,
          onClick: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          Row(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                  .background(InkSurface)
                  .clickable(role = Role.Button, onClick = onClick)
                  .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
          ) {
              Box(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .size(38.dp)
                      .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                      .background(InkSurfaceHigh),
                  contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
              ) {
                  Icon(
                      painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_lock_shield),
                      contentDescription = null,
                      tint = InkAccent,
                      modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp),
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
              Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                  Text(
                      text = "Accountability partner",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                      color = InkTextPrimary,
                  )
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = partnerPhone ?: "Add the person who approves unlock codes.",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = if (partnerPhone == null) "Add" else "Change",
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                  color = InkAccent,
              )
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun PartnerDialog(
          partnerPhone: String?,
          onSave: (String) -> Unit,
          onDismiss: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          var phone by remember(partnerPhone) { mutableStateOf(partnerPhone.orEmpty()) }
          val normalized = phone.trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
          val isChange = partnerPhone != null
      
          Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
              Column(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .fillMaxWidth()
                      .clip(RoundedCornerShape(26.dp))
                      .background(InkSurface)
                      .padding(24.dp),
              ) {
                  Text(
                      text = if (isChange) "Change partner" else "Add accountability partner",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall,
                      color = InkTextPrimary,
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
                  Text(
                      text = "Unlocks and settings changes need a one-time code sent to this number.",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
      
                  OutlinedTextField(
                      value = phone,
                      onValueChange = { phone = it },
                      modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                      label = { Text("Phone number") },
                      placeholder = { Text("+1 555 123 4567") },
                      singleLine = true,
                      keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(
                          keyboardType = KeyboardType.Phone,
                          imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
                      ),
                      shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                      colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                          focusedBorderColor = InkAccent,
                          unfocusedBorderColor = InkHairline,
                          focusedLabelColor = InkAccent,
                          unfocusedLabelColor = InkTextTertiary,
                          focusedTextColor = InkTextPrimary,
                          unfocusedTextColor = InkTextPrimary,
                          cursorColor = InkAccent,
                          focusedContainerColor = InkSurfaceHigh,
                          unfocusedContainerColor = InkSurfaceHigh,
                      ),
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
                  Text(
                      text = when {
                          phone.isNotBlank() && normalized == null ->
                              "Include a country code, for example +1 555 123 4567."
                          isChange ->
                              "Changing an existing partner needs their approval."
                          else ->
                              "The first partner can be saved directly. Later changes need a code."
                      },
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                      color = if (phone.isNotBlank() && normalized == null) InkDanger else InkTextTertiary,
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
      
                  Row(
                      modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                      horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End,
                      verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                  ) {
                      TextButton(
                          onClick = onDismiss,
                          colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = InkTextSecondary),
                      ) {
                          Text("Cancel", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                      }
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
      
                      Button(
                          onClick = { normalized?.let(onSave) },
                          enabled = normalized != null,
                          shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                          colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                              containerColor = InkAccent,
                              contentColor = InkOnAccent,
                              disabledContainerColor = InkSurfaceHigh,
                              disabledContentColor = InkTextTertiary,
                          ),
                      ) {
                          Text("Save", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun Footer(
          ready: Boolean,
          notificationsGranted: Boolean,
          onRequestNotifications: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          Column {
              Hairline()
              Spacer(Modifier.height(22.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = "HOW IT WORKS",
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                  color = InkTextTertiary,
                  letterSpacing = 1.4.sp,
              )
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
      
              listOf(
                  "Only the time Instagram is really on screen counts.",
                  "When the allowance runs out, a strict lock screen appears.",
                  "Your PIN opens Instagram for five minutes.",
                  "Everything resets at midnight; the limit itself stays.",
              ).forEach { line ->
                  Row(modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 3.dp)) {
                      Box(
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .padding(top = 7.dp)
                              .size(3.dp)
                              .clip(CircleShape)
                              .background(InkTextTertiary),
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(9.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = line,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                          color = InkTextTertiary,
                      )
                  }
              }
      
              if (ready && !notificationsGranted) {
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                  TextButton(
                      onClick = onRequestNotifications,
                      colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = InkTextSecondary),
                  ) {
                      Icon(
                          imageVector = Icons.Filled.Notifications,
                          contentDescription = null,
                          modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp),
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                      Text("Enable protection alerts", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                  }
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
      
              Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                  Icon(
                      imageVector = Icons.Filled.Info,
                      contentDescription = null,
                      tint = InkTextTertiary,
                      modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp),
                  )
                  Spacer(Modifier.width(7.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = "Codes expire after 5 minutes",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                      color = InkTextTertiary,
                      letterSpacing = 0.4.sp,
                  )
              }
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Small primitives
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun SectionLabel(text: String) {
          Text(
              text = text,
              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
              color = InkTextTertiary,
              letterSpacing = 1.4.sp,
          )
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun Hairline() {
          HorizontalDivider(color = InkHairline)
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun InlineNotice(
          icon: ImageVector,
          text: String,
          color: Color,
          container: Color,
      ) {
          Row(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                  .background(container)
                  .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
          ) {
              Icon(
                  imageVector = icon,
                  contentDescription = null,
                  tint = color,
                  modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp),
              )
              Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
              Text(
                  text = text,
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                  color = color,
              )
          }
      }
```

```
Act as a Senior Android Security & Architecture Engineer using Kotlin, Jetpack Compose, and modern Android development practices.

I want to significantly upgrade my screen-time blocking app ("ShayLock") to eliminate the vulnerability of a hardcoded PIN, introduce progressive social accountability (WhatsApp/SMS deep-linking), add bulletproof service resilience, and overhaul the user interface to follow a sleek, minimalist dark-mode design (similar to modern apps like Instagram).

### 1. Security & Authentication (Replace PIN with Accountability Partner Flow)
- Remove the hardcoded plaintext PIN ("1234").
- Implement `AccountabilityController.kt`:
  - When the user requests an unlock, generate a single-use 4-digit code that expires in 5 minutes and store it securely in local memory.
  - Automatically format a deep-link intent to open WhatsApp (or fallback to SMS) pre-filled with a message to a saved partner phone number containing the temporary code (e.g., "Hey! I ran out of my Instagram time on ShayLock. If you approve, reply with this code: [XXXX]").
  - Provide validation logic so the user must enter the code sent back by their partner to grant a temporary 5-minute session.
- Add a partner phone number field in the setup/dashboard preferences stored via `SharedPreferences`.

### 2. Robustness & Anti-Tamper Protection
- Accessibility Service Watchdog & WorkManager:
  - Implement a periodic `PeriodicWorkRequest` via `WorkManager` (running every 15 minutes) to verify if `BlockerAccessibilityService` is still enabled in system settings. If disabled while a block rule is active, trigger a high-priority heads-up notification directing the user straight to `Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS`.
- Process Death Resilience:
  - Ensure the background foreground service (`OverlayService`) and `BlockController` properly re-initialize state on process restart using `SharedPreferences` so timers and daily allowances survive app kills or OEM background limits.

### 3. UI/UX Overhaul (Progressive Disclosure & Modern Dark Aesthetics)
```

```
      package com.example.shaylock.core
      
      import android.content.Context
      import android.content.SharedPreferences
      import android.util.Log
      import androidx.core.content.edit
      import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
      import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
      import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
      import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
      import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
      import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
      import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
      import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
      import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.combine
      import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
      import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
      
      /**
       * Process-wide source of truth for the blocking state.
       *
       * The accessibility watchdog, the overlay service and the setup UI all live in the same process,
       * so a single object holding the state keeps them perfectly in sync without any IPC.
       *
       * The model is a *daily allowance*: the user picks how much time the blocked apps may be used
       * per day, the watchdog measures how much of that allowance is actually consumed and, once the
       * allowance is used up, the blocker activates until the next day.
       *
       * Every change to the allowance - including switching the limiter off - requires the PIN.
       *
       * State is persisted to [SharedPreferences] because the allowance, today's usage and an in-flight
       * unlock session must survive the app being killed.
       */
      object BlockController {
      
          private const val TAG = "BlockController"
          private const val PREFS_NAME = "shaylock_blocker"
      
          private const val KEY_LIMIT_ENABLED = "limit_enabled"
          private const val KEY_LIMIT_MS = "limit_ms"
          private const val KEY_USED_MS = "used_ms"
          private const val KEY_DAY_START = "day_start"
          private const val KEY_USAGE_SINCE = "usage_since"
          private const val KEY_SESSION_END = "session_end"
      
          /** How often the running foreground time is written to disk. */
          private const val CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL_MS = 5_000L
      
          /** Long lived scope for the internal clock ticker. */
          private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Default)
      
          private lateinit var prefs: SharedPreferences
      
          private val _limit = MutableStateFlow(DailyLimit())
          private val _sessionEndWallClock = MutableStateFlow(0L)
          private val _now = MutableStateFlow(System.currentTimeMillis())
      
          /** Wall clock at which the current foreground session of a blocked app started; 0 = idle. */
          private val _usageSince = MutableStateFlow(0L)
      
          /** The app that was last reported as being in the foreground. */
          private val _foregroundPackage = MutableStateFlow<String?>(null)
      
          /** True while the lock screen covers the blocked app, so no usage may be counted. */
          private val _usageSuppressed = MutableStateFlow(false)
      
          /** The configured daily allowance together with today's committed usage. */
          val dailyLimit: StateFlow<DailyLimit> = _limit.asStateFlow()
      
          /** Time consumed today, including the currently running foreground session. */
          val usedTodayMs: StateFlow<Long> = combine(_limit, _usageSince, _now) { limit, since, now ->
              limit.usedMs + if (since > 0L) (now - since).coerceAtLeast(0L) else 0L
          }.stateIn(scope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, 0L)
      
          /** Milliseconds of today's allowance that are still available. */
          val limitRemainingMs: StateFlow<Long> = combine(_limit, usedTodayMs) { limit, used ->
              (limit.limitMs - used).coerceAtLeast(0L)
          }.stateIn(scope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, 0L)
      
          /** True when today's allowance is used up. */
          val isLimitReached: StateFlow<Boolean> = combine(_limit, usedTodayMs) { limit, used ->
              limit.isReached(used)
          }.stateIn(scope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, false)
      
          /** Milliseconds left in the PIN-approved unlock session; 0 when no session is running. */
          val sessionRemainingMs: StateFlow<Long> = combine(_sessionEndWallClock, _now) { end, now ->
              if (end <= 0L) 0L else (end - now).coerceAtLeast(0L)
          }.stateIn(scope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, 0L)
      
          /**
           * True when the blocked apps must currently be blocked.
           *
           * Blocking is effective when today's allowance is used up *and* no unlock session is running.
           */
          val isBlockingEffective: StateFlow<Boolean> =
              combine(isLimitReached, _sessionEndWallClock, _now) { reached, sessionEnd, now ->
                  reached && (sessionEnd <= 0L || now >= sessionEnd)
              }.stateIn(scope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, false)
      
          init {
              // Ticks once a second so countdowns stay accurate without every caller owning a timer.
              scope.launch {
                  var lastCheckpoint = 0L
                  while (true) {
                      val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
                      _now.value = now
                      rolloverIfNeeded(now)
                      if (_usageSince.value > 0L && now - lastCheckpoint >= CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL_MS) {
                          lastCheckpoint = now
                          commitUsage(now)
                      }
                      delay(1_000L)
                  }
              }
          }
      
          /**
           * Loads persisted state. Safe to call repeatedly; only the first call has an effect.
           */
          fun initialize(context: Context) {
              if (::prefs.isInitialized) return
              prefs = context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
      
              val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
              val stored = DailyLimit(
                  isEnabled = prefs.getBoolean(KEY_LIMIT_ENABLED, false),
                  limitMs = prefs.getLong(KEY_LIMIT_MS, 0L),
                  usedMs = prefs.getLong(KEY_USED_MS, 0L),
                  dayStartWallClock = prefs.getLong(KEY_DAY_START, 0L),
              )
              val rolled = stored.rolledToDay(now)
              _limit.value = rolled
      
              // A running foreground session survives a process death: its start time was persisted.
              val storedSince = prefs.getLong(KEY_USAGE_SINCE, 0L)
              _usageSince.value =
                  if (storedSince > 0L) maxOf(storedSince, rolled.dayStartWallClock) else 0L
      
              val storedSessionEnd = prefs.getLong(KEY_SESSION_END, 0L)
              if (storedSessionEnd in 1 until now) {
                  // The session expired while the process was dead - the block is active again.
                  _sessionEndWallClock.value = 0L
                  prefs.edit { putLong(KEY_SESSION_END, 0L) }
              } else {
                  _sessionEndWallClock.value = storedSessionEnd
              }
          }
      
          // ----------------------------------------------------------------- usage tracking
      
          /**
           * Called by the watchdog whenever the foreground app changes.
           *
           * Only a blocked app that is really on screen consumes the allowance; the counter is paused
           * as soon as the user leaves the app and resumes when they come back.
           */
          fun onForegroundAppChanged(packageName: String?) {
              _foregroundPackage.value = packageName
              val now = _now.value
              if (shouldCountUsage(packageName)) startCounting(now) else stopCounting(now)
          }
      
          /**
           * Called when the lock screen covers (or uncovers) the blocked app.
           *
           * While the lock screen is up the user is not looking at the blocked app, so that time must
           * not be charged to the daily allowance.
           */
          fun setUsageCountingSuppressed(suppressed: Boolean) {
              if (_usageSuppressed.value == suppressed) return
              _usageSuppressed.value = suppressed
              val now = _now.value
              if (suppressed) {
                  stopCounting(now)
              } else if (shouldCountUsage(_foregroundPackage.value)) {
                  startCounting(now)
              }
          }
      
          private fun shouldCountUsage(packageName: String?): Boolean =
              _limit.value.isEnabled &&
                  !_usageSuppressed.value &&
                  BlockPolicy.isBlockedPackage(packageName)
      
          private fun startCounting(now: Long) {
              if (_usageSince.value > 0L) return
              _usageSince.value = now
              persist()
              Log.d(TAG, "Usage counting started (${_limit.value.usedMs} ms committed so far)")
          }
      
          private fun stopCounting(now: Long) {
              if (_usageSince.value <= 0L) return
              commitUsage(now)
              _usageSince.value = 0L
              persist()
              Log.d(TAG, "Usage counting paused (${_limit.value.usedMs} ms committed)")
          }
      
          /** Moves the running foreground time into the committed usage counter. */
          private fun commitUsage(now: Long) {
              val since = _usageSince.value
              if (since <= 0L) return
              val elapsed = (now - since).coerceAtLeast(0L)
              val limit = _limit.value
              _limit.value = limit.copy(usedMs = limit.usedMs + elapsed)
              _usageSince.value = now
              persist()
          }
      
          /** Resets the usage counter when the calendar day changed. */
          private fun rolloverIfNeeded(now: Long) {
              val current = _limit.value
              val rolled = current.rolledToDay(now)
              if (rolled === current) return
      
              _limit.value = rolled
              // A session that started before midnight only counts from the new day onwards.
              val since = _usageSince.value
              if (since > 0L) _usageSince.value = maxOf(since, rolled.dayStartWallClock)
              persist()
              Log.i(TAG, "New day - today's usage counter reset")
          }
      
          // ----------------------------------------------------------------- PIN gated actions
      
          /**
           * Checks the PIN without granting anything.
           *
           * Used by every setting that the user must not be able to change without the PIN.
           */
          fun authorize(pin: String): Boolean = matchesPin(pin)
      
          /**
           * Validates a PIN entry for the lock screen.
           *
           * @return true when the PIN matched, in which case a five-minute unlock session was started.
           */
          fun submitPin(code: String): Boolean {
              if (!matchesPin(code)) return false
      
              grantUnlockSession(BlockPolicy.UNLOCK_SESSION_MS)
              Log.i(TAG, "Correct PIN - unlock session granted")
              return true
          }
      
          private fun matchesPin(code: String): Boolean {
              val accepted = BlockPolicy.isCorrectPin(code)
              Log.i(TAG, "PIN check for ${code.length} digits -> ${if (accepted) "accepted" else "rejected"}")
              return accepted
          }
      
          /**
           * True when the daily allowance has already been configured at least once.
           *
           * The very first setup is deliberately free so the blocker can be armed without knowing the
           * PIN; every later change (including re-arming after switching it off) needs the PIN.
           */
          fun isPinRequiredToSetLimit(): Boolean =
              _limit.value.isEnabled || _limit.value.limitMs > 0L
      
          /**
           * Sets the daily allowance and switches the limiter on.
           *
           * The allowance stays exactly as set until it is changed again with the PIN.
           *
           * @param pin the PIN; null is only accepted while no allowance has been configured yet.
           * @return true when the allowance was applied (either first setup or a correct PIN).
           */
          fun applyDailyLimit(limitMs: Long, pin: String?): Boolean {
              val firstSetup = !isPinRequiredToSetLimit()
              if (!firstSetup && (pin == null || !authorize(pin))) return false
      
              val rolled = _limit.value.rolledToDay(_now.value)
              _limit.value = rolled.copy(isEnabled = true, limitMs = limitMs.coerceAtLeast(0L))
              persist()
              Log.i(TAG, "Daily allowance set to ${_limit.value.limitMs} ms (first setup=$firstSetup)")
              return true
          }
      
          /**
           * Switches the limiter on or off.
           *
           * Switching it off requires the PIN as well - there is no free way to disable the blocker.
           *
           * @return true when the PIN was correct and the limiter was switched.
           */
          fun setLimiterEnabled(enabled: Boolean, pin: String): Boolean {
              if (!authorize(pin)) return false
      
              if (enabled) {
                  _limit.value = _limit.value.copy(isEnabled = true)
              } else {
                  stopCounting(_now.value)
                  _limit.value = _limit.value.copy(isEnabled = false)
                  endSession()
              }
              persist()
              Log.i(TAG, "Limiter enabled=$enabled")
              return true
          }
      
          /**
           * Clears today's usage so the full allowance is available again.
           *
           * @return true when the PIN was correct and the counter was reset.
           */
          fun resetTodayUsage(pin: String): Boolean {
              if (!authorize(pin)) return false
      
              stopCounting(_now.value)
              val rolled = _limit.value.rolledToDay(_now.value)
              _limit.value = rolled.copy(usedMs = 0L)
              _usageSince.value = 0L
              persist()
              Log.i(TAG, "Today's usage counter reset")
              return true
          }
      
          // ----------------------------------------------------------------- unlock session
      
          /** Starts the temporary unlock session that lifts the block for [durationMs]. */
          fun grantUnlockSession(durationMs: Long) {
              val end = System.currentTimeMillis() + durationMs
              _sessionEndWallClock.value = end
              prefs.edit { putLong(KEY_SESSION_END, end) }
          }
      
          /**
           * Temporarily suspends blocking for [durationMs] after verifying the PIN.
           *
           * The pause reuses the unlock session, so usage keeps counting and the block returns as soon
           * as the session ends and the allowance is still exhausted.
           *
           * @return true when the PIN was correct and the pause was granted.
           */
          fun pauseFor(durationMs: Long, pin: String): Boolean {
              if (!authorize(pin)) return false
      
              grantUnlockSession(durationMs)
              Log.i(TAG, "Pause granted for $durationMs ms")
              return true
          }
      
          /** Immediately terminates any running unlock session, re-arming the block. */
          fun endSession() {
              _sessionEndWallClock.value = 0L
              prefs.edit { putLong(KEY_SESSION_END, 0L) }
          }
      
          /** Convenience snapshot for callers that do not observe flows. */
          fun isUnlockSessionActive(): Boolean = sessionRemainingMs.value > 0L
      
          private fun persist() {
              if (!::prefs.isInitialized) return
              val limit = _limit.value
              prefs.edit {
                  putBoolean(KEY_LIMIT_ENABLED, limit.isEnabled)
                  putLong(KEY_LIMIT_MS, limit.limitMs)
                  putLong(KEY_USED_MS, limit.usedMs)
                  putLong(KEY_DAY_START, limit.dayStartWallClock)
                  putLong(KEY_USAGE_SINCE, _usageSince.value)
              }
          }
      }
      
- path: app\src\main\java\com\example\shaylock\service\BlockerAccessibilityService.kt
  content: |
      package com.example.shaylock.service
      
      import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService
      import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
      import android.content.Context
      import android.content.Intent
      import android.content.IntentFilter
      import android.os.Build
      import android.util.Log
      import android.view.KeyEvent
      import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent
      import android.view.inputmethod.InputMethodManager
      import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
      import com.example.shaylock.core.BlockController
      import com.example.shaylock.core.BlockPolicy
      import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
      import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
      import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
      import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
      import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
      
      /**
       * The blocker watchdog.
       *
       * Responsibilities:
       *  1. Observe foreground window changes and detect when a blocked app (Instagram) is opened.
       *  2. Measure how much of the daily allowance is actually consumed - only the time the blocked
       *     app is really on screen is charged to the allowance.
       *  3. Raise the full-screen lock overlay over that app as soon as the allowance is used up.
       *  4. Swallow the HOME / BACK / APP-SWITCH key events while the overlay is on screen so the
       *     user cannot simply navigate around the lock.
       *
       * The service is declared with `BIND_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICE` in the manifest and configured by
       * `res/xml/accessibility_service_config.xml`.
       */
      class BlockerAccessibilityService : AccessibilityService() {
      
          private var scope: CoroutineScope? = null
      
          /** The package that was last reported as being in the foreground. */
          private var foregroundPackage: String? = null
      
          /**
           * Windows that can appear over the blocked app without replacing it.
           *
           * Their events must never become the "foreground app" used for the block decision, and they
           * must not pause usage counting either: the blocked app is still behind them and the system
           * does not always deliver a new window-state event when they disappear. That combination used
           * to pause the timer (e.g. while watching reels) until the next Instagram UI change.
           */
          private val transientPackages = mutableSetOf("com.android.systemui")
      
          /** True while the screen-state receiver is registered. */
          private var screenReceiverRegistered = false
      
          /**
           * Stops the allowance while the screen is off. Transient system-UI events no longer pause the
           * counter, so the keyguard must be handled explicitly.
           */
          private val screenReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
              override fun onReceive(context: Context?, intent: Intent?) {
                  when (intent?.action) {
                      Intent.ACTION_SCREEN_OFF -> {
                          Log.d(TAG, "Screen off - pausing usage counting")
                          BlockController.onForegroundAppChanged(null)
                      }
      
                      Intent.ACTION_SCREEN_ON,
                      Intent.ACTION_USER_PRESENT,
                      -> resumeFromCurrentWindow()
                  }
              }
          }
      
          override fun onServiceConnected() {
              super.onServiceConnected()
              instance = this
              scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.Main.immediate)
              BlockController.initialize(applicationContext)
      
              // The keyboard is a separate window; its events must not end the blocked app's session.
              getSystemService(InputMethodManager::class.java)
                  ?.enabledInputMethodList
                  ?.forEach { transientPackages += it.packageName }
      
              registerScreenReceiver()
      
              Log.i(TAG, "Blocker watchdog connected")
      
              // The block has to appear the moment the allowance runs out, even if the user never
              // leaves the app, and it has to come back when the unlock session ends.
              scope?.launch {
                  BlockController.isBlockingEffective.collect {
                      evaluateBlocking(foregroundPackage)
                  }
              }
      
              // Pick up the app that is already on screen when the service is (re)connected.
              resumeFromCurrentWindow()
      
              // If the allowance was already used up when the service was (re)connected, pick it up.
              evaluateBlocking(foregroundPackage)
          }
      
          override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {
              // TYPE_WINDOWS_CHANGED fires for every window anywhere (status bar, keyboard, toasts) and
              // is not a reliable "this app came to the foreground" signal. Only a window-state change
              // means an app actually became the foreground window.
              if (event?.eventType != AccessibilityEvent.TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED) return
      
              val packageName = event.packageName?.toString() ?: return
      
              // Ignore events produced by our own overlay window.
              if (packageName == this.packageName) return
      
              // System UI and the keyboard are transient windows drawn over the app: keep the real app
              // as the blocking target and keep charging the allowance. Do not rely on a later event to
              // resume counting - while watching reels no new window-state event may arrive.
              if (packageName in transientPackages) return
      
              BlockController.onForegroundAppChanged(packageName)
      
              if (packageName != foregroundPackage) {
                  Log.d(TAG, "Foreground app -> $packageName")
                  foregroundPackage = packageName
              }
      
              evaluateBlocking(packageName)
          }
      
          /** Registers the receiver that pauses counting while the screen is off. */
          private fun registerScreenReceiver() {
              if (screenReceiverRegistered) return
              val filter = IntentFilter().apply {
                  addAction(Intent.ACTION_SCREEN_OFF)
                  addAction(Intent.ACTION_SCREEN_ON)
                  addAction(Intent.ACTION_USER_PRESENT)
              }
              ContextCompat.registerReceiver(
                  this,
                  screenReceiver,
                  filter,
                  ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED,
              )
              screenReceiverRegistered = true
          }
      
          private fun unregisterScreenReceiver() {
              if (!screenReceiverRegistered) return
              runCatching { unregisterReceiver(screenReceiver) }
              screenReceiverRegistered = false
          }
      
          /**
           * Reads the app that is actually on screen and resumes counting for it.
           *
           * Used after the screen turns back on and when the service reconnects, because neither case
           * guarantees a new accessibility window-state event.
           */
          private fun resumeFromCurrentWindow() {
              val current = rootInActiveWindow?.packageName?.toString() ?: return
              if (current == this.packageName || current in transientPackages) return
      
              Log.d(TAG, "Current foreground window -> $current")
              foregroundPackage = current
              BlockController.onForegroundAppChanged(current)
              evaluateBlocking(current)
          }
      
          /**
           * Filters the global key stream while the lock overlay is visible.
           *
           * Returning `true` consumes the event so the system never acts on it. The guard on
           * [OverlayService.isOverlayShowing] is what keeps the rest of the device usable: outside of a
           * block the keys are passed straight through.
           *
           * Note: only one accessibility service can hold the key-filtering capability at a time on a
           * given device, so this may be unavailable if the user has another key-filtering service
           * enabled. The watchdog re-arms the overlay whenever the blocked app returns to the
           * foreground, which is the fallback enforcement path.
           */
          override fun onKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
              if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return false
              if (!OverlayService.isOverlayShowing) return false
              if (BlockController.isUnlockSessionActive()) return false
      
              return when (event.keyCode) {
                  KeyEvent.KEYCODE_HOME,
                  KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BACK,
                  KeyEvent.KEYCODE_APP_SWITCH,
                  -> true // Consumed: the user cannot navigate away from the lock screen.
      
                  else -> false
              }
          }
      
          /**
           * Re-evaluates the app that is currently on screen.
           *
           * Used when the overlay service is recreated after a process death: there may be no fresh
           * accessibility event, but the blocked app can already be visible.
           */
          fun recheckForeground() {
              resumeFromCurrentWindow()
          }
      
          override fun onInterrupt() {
              Log.i(TAG, "Blocker watchdog interrupted")
          }
      
          override fun onDestroy() {
              unregisterScreenReceiver()
              OverlayController.hide(this)
              scope?.cancel()
              scope = null
              instance = null
              super.onDestroy()
          }
      
          override fun onUnbind(intent: Intent?): Boolean {
              unregisterScreenReceiver()
              OverlayController.hide(this)
              instance = null
              return super.onUnbind(intent)
          }
      
          /**
           * Decides whether the currently visible app must be covered by the lock overlay.
           *
           * @param packageName the app reported in the foreground, or null when unknown.
           */
          private fun evaluateBlocking(packageName: String?) {
              val target = packageName ?: return
      
              val shouldBlock =
                  BlockPolicy.isBlockedPackage(target) &&
                      BlockController.isBlockingEffective.value
      
              if (shouldBlock) {
                  if (!OverlayService.isOverlayShowing) {
                      val used = BlockController.usedTodayMs.value
                      val limit = BlockController.dailyLimit.value.limitMs
                      Log.i(TAG, "Blocking $target - daily allowance used up ($used / $limit ms)")
                      OverlayController.show(this, target)
                  }
                  // The user is looking at the lock screen, not at the app: stop charging the allowance.
                  BlockController.setUsageCountingSuppressed(true)
              } else {
                  if (OverlayService.isOverlayShowing) {
                      Log.i(TAG, "Releasing overlay")
                      OverlayController.hide(this)
                  }
                  BlockController.setUsageCountingSuppressed(false)
              }
          }
      
          companion object {
              private const val TAG = "BlockerA11y"
      
              @Volatile
              var instance: BlockerAccessibilityService? = null
                  private set
      
              /** True once the system has connected the service and it is ready to watch. */
              val isRunning: Boolean
                  get() = instance != null
          }
      }
      
```

````
## Objective
- Upgrade ShayLock from a hardcoded PIN blocker to a partner-accountability blocker using expiring one-time codes delivered via WhatsApp/SMS deep links.
- Add WorkManager-based accessibility watchdog checks, improve process-death/service resilience, and finish the modern progressive dark-mode UI.

## Important Details
- Hardcoded PIN `1234` must be removed. Protected actions now require a partner approval code instead.
- One-time codes: 4 digits, expire after 5 minutes, stored only in memory, single-use.
- First daily-limit setup is still allowed without approval; later limit changes require `CHANGE_LIMIT`.
- First partner phone save is direct; changing an existing partner requires `CHANGE_PARTNER`.
- Partner number is stored in `SharedPreferences` under `shaylock_accountability`.
- Deep-link flow is accountability friction, not cryptographic security: the code is pre-filled in the WhatsApp/SMS message, so the device owner can read it. A real product would need a backend.
- WorkManager dependency `androidx.work:work-runtime-ktx:2.9.1` is available in the Gradle cache.
- Periodic health check interval is 15 minutes. If a rule is enabled but accessibility is disabled in settings, show a high-priority notification opening `Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS`.
- Project constraints: `minSdk 26`, `targetSdk 35`, Kotlin/Compose, offline Gradle build.
- Build command used previously:
  `$env:JAVA_HOME="C:\Program Files\Eclipse Adoptium\jdk-17.0.19.10-hotspot"; .\gradlew.bat :app:assembleDebug :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:lintDebug --offline --console=plain`
````
