      /**
       * The single dashboard / onboarding screen.
       *
       * The screen reveals itself progressively:
       *  1. only missing required permissions are shown,
       *  2. once they are granted the permission section collapses and the allowance picker appears,
       *  3. once an allowance is armed the picker gives way to the focused active dashboard.
       */
      @Composable
      fun SetupScreen(
          modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
          permissionState: PermissionUiState,
          limit: DailyLimit,
          usedTodayMs: Long,
          limitRemainingMs: Long,
          isBlockingEffective: Boolean,
          sessionRemainingMs: Long,
          selectedLimitMs: Long,
          onSelectLimit: (Long) -> Unit,
          onRequestSetLimit: (Long) -> Unit,
          onRequestDisable: () -> Unit,
          onRequestResetUsage: () -> Unit,
          onRequestPause: () -> Unit,
          onResumeNow: () -> Unit,
          onOpenAccessibilitySettings: () -> Unit,
          onOpenOverlaySettings: () -> Unit,
          onRequestNotifications: () -> Unit,
          onPreviewOverlay: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          val ready = permissionState.readyToBlock
          val isDashboard = ready && limit.isEnabled
          val showPermissions = !ready
          val showAllowanceSetup = ready && !limit.isEnabled
          val showDashboard = isDashboard
      
          var showLimitEditor by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
          LaunchedEffect(limit.limitMs, limit.isEnabled) {
              if (limit.isEnabled) showLimitEditor = false
          }
      
          Column(
              modifier = modifier
                  .fillMaxSize()
                  .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                  .padding(start = 20.dp, end = 20.dp, top = 20.dp, bottom = 40.dp),
          ) {
              ScreenHeader(
                  isActive = isDashboard,
                  isPaused = sessionRemainingMs > 0L,
              )
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(26.dp))
      
              StatusHero(
                  permissionState = permissionState,
                  limit = limit,
                  usedTodayMs = usedTodayMs,
                  limitRemainingMs = limitRemainingMs,
                  isBlockingEffective = isBlockingEffective,
                  sessionRemainingMs = sessionRemainingMs,
              )
      
              if (!permissionState.targetInstalled) {
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                  InlineNotice(
                      icon = Icons.Filled.Warning,
                      text = "Instagram does not appear to be installed, so there is nothing to " +
                          "block yet.",
                      color = InkWarning,
                      container = InkWarningContainer,
                  )
              }
      
              /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
               * Permissions - only while something is missing
               * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
              AnimatedVisibility(
                  visible = showPermissions,
                  enter = fadeIn(tween(260)) + expandVertically(tween(320)),
                  exit = fadeOut(tween(180)) + shrinkVertically(tween(280)),
              ) {
                  Column {
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(30.dp))
      
                      SectionLabel("GET PROTECTED")
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
      
                      if (!permissionState.accessibilityEnabled) {
                          PermissionRow(
                              icon = Icons.Filled.Settings,
                              title = "Accessibility service",
                              description = "Lets ShayLock notice when Instagram is on screen and " +
                                  "measure the time it is actually used.",
                              status = if (permissionState.accessibilityConnecting) {
                                  PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING
                              } else {
                                  PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED
                              },
                              steps = listOf(
                                  "Open Settings → Accessibility.",
                                  "Tap ShayLock under Installed services.",
                                  "Turn on \"Use service\" and confirm the prompt.",
                              ),
                              actionLabel = "Open settings",
                              onAction = onOpenAccessibilitySettings,
                          )
      
                          if (!permissionState.overlayGranted ||
                              !permissionState.notificationsGranted
                          ) {
                              Hairline()
                          }
                      }
      
                      if (!permissionState.overlayGranted) {
                          PermissionRow(
                              icon = Icons.Filled.Warning,
                              title = "Display over other apps",
                              description = "Allows the full-screen lock screen to cover Instagram.",
                              status = PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED,
                              steps = listOf(
                                  "Open Settings → Apps → ShayLock.",
                                  "Tap \"Display over other apps\".",
                                  "Turn the switch on.",
                              ),
                              actionLabel = "Open settings",
                              onAction = onOpenOverlaySettings,
                          )
      
                          if (!permissionState.notificationsGranted) {
                              Hairline()
                          }
                      }
      
                      if (!permissionState.notificationsGranted) {
                          PermissionRow(
                              icon = Icons.Filled.Notifications,
                              title = "Notifications",
                              description = "Optional. Shows the ongoing protection notification.",
                              status = PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL,
                              steps = listOf("Tap \"Allow\" on the Android prompt."),
                              actionLabel = "Allow",
                              onAction = onRequestNotifications,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
      
              /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
               * Allowance setup - once both permissions are active
               * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
              AnimatedVisibility(
                  visible = showAllowanceSetup,
                  enter = fadeIn(tween(260)) + expandVertically(tween(320)),
                  exit = fadeOut(tween(180)) + shrinkVertically(tween(280)),
              ) {
                  Column {
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(30.dp))
      
                      SectionLabel("DAILY ALLOWANCE")
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
      
                      Text(
                          text = "Choose how much Instagram is allowed each day.",
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                          color = InkTextPrimary,
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = "Only time Instagram is really on screen counts.",
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                          color = InkTextSecondary,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
      
                      AllowanceChips(
                          selected = selectedLimitMs,
                          onSelect = onSelectLimit,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
      
                      Button(
                          onClick = { onRequestSetLimit(selectedLimitMs) },
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .fillMaxWidth()
                              .height(54.dp),
                          shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                          colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                              containerColor = InkAccent,
                              contentColor = InkOnAccent,
                          ),
                      ) {
                          Image(
                              painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_shaylock_mark),
                              contentDescription = null,
                              modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp),
                          )
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                          Text("Start blocking", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                      }
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = "The first setup needs no PIN. Every later change needs the PIN.",
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                          color = InkTextTertiary,
                      )
                  }
              }
      
              /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
               * Active dashboard
               * ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
              AnimatedVisibility(
                  visible = showDashboard,
                  enter = fadeIn(tween(260)) + expandVertically(tween(320)),
                  exit = fadeOut(tween(180)) + shrinkVertically(tween(280)),
              ) {
                  Column {
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(30.dp))
      
                      SectionLabel("CONTROLS")
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
      
                      when {
                          sessionRemainingMs > 0L -> {
                              Button(
                                  onClick = onResumeNow,
                                  modifier = Modifier
                                      .fillMaxWidth()
                                      .height(52.dp),
                                  shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                                  colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                      containerColor = InkAccent,
                                      contentColor = InkOnAccent,
                                  ),
                              ) {
                                  Text("Resume blocking", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                              }
                          }
      
                          isBlockingEffective -> {
                              Button(
                                  onClick = onRequestPause,
                                  modifier = Modifier
                                      .fillMaxWidth()
                                      .height(52.dp),
                                  shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                                  colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                      containerColor = InkWarningContainer,
                                      contentColor = InkOnWarningContainer,
                                  ),
                              ) {
                                  Icon(
                                      imageVector = Icons.Filled.Warning,
                                      contentDescription = null,
                                      modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp),
                                  )
                                  Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                  Text("Pause for 15 minutes", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                              }
                          }
                      }
      
                      if (sessionRemainingMs > 0L || isBlockingEffective) {
                          Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
                      }
      
                      ActionRow(
                          title = "Daily allowance",
                          subtitle = "Currently ${formatLimit(limit.limitMs)} per day",
                          action = if (showLimitEditor) "Close" else "Change",
                          onClick = { showLimitEditor = !showLimitEditor },
                      )
      
                      AnimatedVisibility(
                          visible = showLimitEditor,
                          enter = fadeIn(tween(200)) + expandVertically(tween(260)),
                          exit = fadeOut(tween(160)) + shrinkVertically(tween(240)),
                      ) {
                          Column {
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
                              AllowanceChips(
                                  selected = selectedLimitMs,
                                  onSelect = onSelectLimit,
                              )
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                              Button(
                                  onClick = { onRequestSetLimit(selectedLimitMs) },
                                  modifier = Modifier
                                      .fillMaxWidth()
                                      .height(48.dp),
                                  shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                                  colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                      containerColor = InkAccentContainer,
                                      contentColor = InkAccent,
                                  ),
                              ) {
                                  Text("Save new limit", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                              }
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
                              Text(
                                  text = "Saving a new limit needs your PIN.",
                                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                  color = InkTextTertiary,
                              )
                              Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
                          }
                      }
      
                      Hairline()
      
                      ActionRow(
                          title = "Reset today's usage",
                          subtitle = "${formatLimit(usedTodayMs)} used today",
                          action = "Reset",
                          onClick = onRequestResetUsage,
                      )
      
                      Hairline()
      
                      ActionRow(
                          title = "Turn off the blocker",
                          subtitle = "Removes the rule until you set it again",
                          action = "Turn off",
                          destructive = true,
                          onClick = onRequestDisable,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
      
                      TextButton(
                          onClick = onPreviewOverlay,
                          modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                      ) {
                          Image(
                              painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_shaylock_mark),
                              contentDescription = null,
                              modifier = Modifier
                                  .size(18.dp)
                                  .alpha(0.7f),
                          )
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                          Text(
                              text = "Preview the lock screen",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                              color = InkTextSecondary,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
      
              if (!ready) {
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = "The Start blocking button appears once both required permissions " +
                          "are active.",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextTertiary,
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(34.dp))
      
              Footer(
                  ready = ready,
                  notificationsGranted = permissionState.notificationsGranted,
                  onRequestNotifications = onRequestNotifications,
              )
      
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Header and hero
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun ScreenHeader(isActive: Boolean, isPaused: Boolean) {
          Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
              Box(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .size(42.dp)
                      .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                      .background(InkAccentContainer),
                  contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
              ) {
                  Image(
                      painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_shaylock_mark),
                      contentDescription = null,
                      modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp),
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
              Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                  Text(
                      text = "ShayLock",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                      color = InkTextPrimary,
                  )
                  Text(
                      text = "Strict screen-time blocker",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                      color = InkTextTertiary,
                      letterSpacing = 0.4.sp,
                  )
              }
      
              val label: String
              val container: Color
              val content: Color
              when {
                  isPaused -> {
                      label = "PAUSED"
                      container = InkWarningContainer
                      content = InkOnWarningContainer
                  }
      
                  isActive -> {
                      label = "ACTIVE"
                      container = InkSuccessContainer
                      content = InkSuccess
                  }
      
                  else -> {
                      label = "SETUP"
                      container = InkSurfaceHigh
                      content = InkTextSecondary
                  }
              }
      
              Row(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .clip(CircleShape)
                      .background(container)
                      .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp),
                  verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
              ) {
                  Box(
                      modifier = Modifier
                          .size(5.dp)
                          .clip(CircleShape)
                          .background(content),
                  )
                  Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = label,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                      color = content,
                  )
              }
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun StatusHero(
          permissionState: PermissionUiState,
          limit: DailyLimit,
          usedTodayMs: Long,
          limitRemainingMs: Long,
          isBlockingEffective: Boolean,
          sessionRemainingMs: Long,
      ) {
          val accent = when {
              !permissionState.readyToBlock &&
                  permissionState.accessibilityConnecting -> InkWarning
              !permissionState.readyToBlock -> InkDanger
              sessionRemainingMs > 0L -> InkWarning
              isBlockingEffective -> InkDanger
              limit.isEnabled -> InkSuccess
              else -> InkAccent
          }
          val animatedAccent by animateColorAsState(
              targetValue = accent,
              animationSpec = tween(400),
              label = "heroAccent",
          )
      
          val eyebrow: String
          val value: String
          val suffix: String
          val detail: String
          when {
              !permissionState.readyToBlock && permissionState.accessibilityConnecting -> {
                  eyebrow = "CONNECTING"
                  value = "Almost there"
                  suffix = ""
                  detail = "Android is finishing the accessibility connection."
              }
      
              !permissionState.readyToBlock -> {
                  eyebrow = "NOT PROTECTED"
                  value = "Setup required"
                  suffix = ""
                  detail = "Grant the missing permissions below to start protecting Instagram."
              }
      
              sessionRemainingMs > 0L -> {
                  eyebrow = "PAUSED"
                  value = formatCountdown(sessionRemainingMs)
                  suffix = "left"
                  detail = "Blocking resumes automatically when the pause ends."
              }
      
              isBlockingEffective -> {
                  eyebrow = "BLOCKED"
                  value = "Locked"
                  suffix = "until midnight"
                  detail = "Today's ${formatLimit(limit.limitMs)} allowance is used up."
              }
      
              limit.isEnabled -> {
                  eyebrow = "PROTECTED"
                  value = formatLimit(limitRemainingMs)
                  suffix = "left today"
                  detail = "Only time Instagram is really on screen counts."
              }
      
              else -> {
                  eyebrow = "READY"
                  value = "No limit yet"
                  suffix = ""
                  detail = "Choose a daily allowance below to arm the blocker."
              }
          }
      
          Box(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(26.dp))
                  .background(InkSurface),
          ) {
              Box(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .matchParentSize()
                      .background(
                          Brush.verticalGradient(
                              colors = listOf(
                                  animatedAccent.copy(alpha = 0.08f),
                                  Color.Transparent,
                              ),
                          ),
                      ),
              )
      
              Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(22.dp)) {
                  Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                      Box(
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .size(5.dp)
                              .clip(CircleShape)
                              .background(animatedAccent),
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(7.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = eyebrow,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                          color = animatedAccent,
                          letterSpacing = 1.4.sp,
                      )
                  }
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
      
                  Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom) {
                      Text(
                          text = value,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.displaySmall,
                          color = InkTextPrimary,
                      )
                      if (suffix.isNotEmpty()) {
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(9.dp))
                          Text(
                              text = suffix,
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                              color = InkTextSecondary,
                              modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 5.dp),
                          )
                      }
                  }
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
      
                  Text(
                      text = detail,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
      
                  if (limit.isEnabled && limit.limitMs > 0L) {
                      val progress by animateFloatAsState(
                          targetValue = limit.usedFraction(usedTodayMs),
                          animationSpec = tween(600),
                          label = "usageProgress",
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
      
                      LinearProgressIndicator(
                          progress = { progress },
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .fillMaxWidth()
                              .height(4.dp)
                              .clip(RoundedCornerShape(50)),
                          color = animatedAccent,
                          trackColor = InkHairline,
                      )
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
      
                      Row {
                          Text(
                              text = "${formatLimit(usedTodayMs)} used",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                              color = InkTextTertiary,
                              letterSpacing = 0.3.sp,
                              modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                          )
                          Text(
                              text = "${formatLimit(limit.limitMs)} per day",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                              color = InkTextTertiary,
                              letterSpacing = 0.3.sp,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Permission rows
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      private enum class PermissionRowStatus(val label: String) {
          CONNECTING("Connecting…"),
          REQUIRED("Action required"),
          OPTIONAL("Optional"),
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun PermissionRow(
          icon: ImageVector,
          title: String,
          description: String,
          status: PermissionRowStatus,
          steps: List<String>,
          actionLabel: String,
          onAction: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          val statusColor = when (status) {
              PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING -> InkWarning
              PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED -> InkDanger
              PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL -> InkTextTertiary
          }
          val iconContainer = when (status) {
              PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING -> InkWarningContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED -> InkDangerContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL -> InkSurfaceHigh
          }
          val iconContent = when (status) {
              PermissionRowStatus.CONNECTING -> InkOnWarningContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.REQUIRED -> InkOnDangerContainer
              PermissionRowStatus.OPTIONAL -> InkTextSecondary
          }
      
          Column(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .padding(vertical = 16.dp),
          ) {
              Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                  Box(
                      modifier = Modifier
                          .size(38.dp)
                          .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                          .background(iconContainer),
                      contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                  ) {
                      Icon(
                          imageVector = icon,
                          contentDescription = null,
                          tint = iconContent,
                          modifier = Modifier.size(19.dp),
                      )
                  }
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
                  Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                      Text(
                          text = title,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                          color = InkTextPrimary,
                      )
                      Text(
                          text = status.label,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                          color = statusColor,
                          letterSpacing = 0.5.sp,
                      )
                  }
      
                  TextButton(
                      onClick = onAction,
                      colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = InkAccent),
                  ) {
                      Text(actionLabel, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                  }
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = description,
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                  color = InkTextSecondary,
                  modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 50.dp),
              )
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
              Column(
                  modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 50.dp),
                  verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp),
              ) {
                  steps.forEachIndexed { index, step ->
                      Row {
                          Text(
                              text = "${index + 1}.",
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                              color = statusColor,
                          )
                          Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                          Text(
                              text = step,
                              style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                              color = InkTextTertiary,
                          )
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Allowance editor
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun AllowanceChips(selected: Long, onSelect: (Long) -> Unit) {
          val options = BlockPolicy.DAILY_LIMIT_OPTIONS
      
          Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
              options.chunked(3).forEach { row ->
                  Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                      row.forEach { option ->
                          val isSelected = selected == option
                          Row(
                              modifier = Modifier
                                  .weight(1f)
                                  .height(46.dp)
                                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                                  .background(if (isSelected) InkAccentContainer else InkSurface)
                                  .clickable(role = Role.RadioButton) { onSelect(option) }
                                  .padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                              horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                          ) {
                              if (isSelected) {
                                  Icon(
                                      imageVector = Icons.Filled.Check,
                                      contentDescription = null,
                                      tint = InkAccent,
                                      modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp),
                                  )
                                  Spacer(Modifier.width(5.dp))
                              }
                              Text(
                                  text = formatLimit(option),
                                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                                  color = if (isSelected) InkAccent else InkTextSecondary,
                              )
                          }
                      }
                      repeat(3 - row.size) {
                          Spacer(Modifier.weight(1f))
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Dashboard action rows and footer
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun ActionRow(
          title: String,
          subtitle: String,
          action: String,
          onClick: () -> Unit,
          destructive: Boolean = false,
      ) {
          Row(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clickable(role = Role.Button, onClick = onClick)
                  .padding(vertical = 17.dp),
              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
          ) {
              Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                  Text(
                      text = title,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                      color = if (destructive) InkDanger else InkTextPrimary,
                  )
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = subtitle,
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = action,
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                  color = if (destructive) InkDanger else InkAccent,
              )
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun PartnerRow(
          partnerPhone: String?,
          onClick: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          Row(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                  .background(InkSurface)
                  .clickable(role = Role.Button, onClick = onClick)
                  .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
              verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
          ) {
              Box(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .size(38.dp)
                      .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                      .background(InkSurfaceHigh),
                  contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
              ) {
                  Image(
                      painter = painterResource(R.drawable.ic_shaylock_mark),
                      contentDescription = null,
                      modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp),
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
      
              Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                  Text(
                      text = "Accountability partner",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                      color = InkTextPrimary,
                  )
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
                  Text(
                      text = partnerPhone ?: "Add the person who approves unlock codes.",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
              }
      
              Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = if (partnerPhone == null) "Add" else "Change",
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                  color = InkAccent,
              )
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun PartnerDialog(
          partnerPhone: String?,
          onSave: (String) -> Unit,
          onDismiss: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          var phone by remember(partnerPhone) { mutableStateOf(partnerPhone.orEmpty()) }
          val normalized = phone.trim().takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
          val isChange = partnerPhone != null
      
          Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
              Column(
                  modifier = Modifier
                      .fillMaxWidth()
                      .clip(RoundedCornerShape(26.dp))
                      .background(InkSurface)
                      .padding(24.dp),
              ) {
                  Text(
                      text = if (isChange) "Change partner" else "Add accountability partner",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall,
                      color = InkTextPrimary,
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
                  Text(
                      text = "Unlocks and settings changes need a one-time code sent to this number.",
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                      color = InkTextSecondary,
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
      
                  OutlinedTextField(
                      value = phone,
                      onValueChange = { phone = it },
                      modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                      label = { Text("Phone number") },
                      placeholder = { Text("+1 555 123 4567") },
                      singleLine = true,
                      keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(
                          keyboardType = KeyboardType.Phone,
                          imeAction = ImeAction.Done,
                      ),
                      shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                      colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
                          focusedBorderColor = InkAccent,
                          unfocusedBorderColor = InkHairline,
                          focusedLabelColor = InkAccent,
                          unfocusedLabelColor = InkTextTertiary,
                          focusedTextColor = InkTextPrimary,
                          unfocusedTextColor = InkTextPrimary,
                          cursorColor = InkAccent,
                          focusedContainerColor = InkSurfaceHigh,
                          unfocusedContainerColor = InkSurfaceHigh,
                      ),
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
      
                  Text(
                      text = when {
                          phone.isNotBlank() && normalized == null ->
                              "Include a country code, for example +1 555 123 4567."
                          isChange ->
                              "Changing an existing partner needs their approval."
                          else ->
                              "The first partner can be saved directly. Later changes need a code."
                      },
                      style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                      color = if (phone.isNotBlank() && normalized == null) InkDanger else InkTextTertiary,
                  )
      
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(20.dp))
      
                  Row(
                      modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                      horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.End,
                      verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                  ) {
                      TextButton(
                          onClick = onDismiss,
                          colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = InkTextSecondary),
                      ) {
                          Text("Cancel", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                      }
      
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
      
                      Button(
                          onClick = { normalized?.let(onSave) },
                          enabled = normalized != null,
                          shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                          colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                              containerColor = InkAccent,
                              contentColor = InkOnAccent,
                              disabledContainerColor = InkSurfaceHigh,
                              disabledContentColor = InkTextTertiary,
                          ),
                      ) {
                          Text("Save", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                      }
                  }
              }
          }
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun Footer(
          ready: Boolean,
          notificationsGranted: Boolean,
          onRequestNotifications: () -> Unit,
      ) {
          Column {
              Hairline()
              Spacer(Modifier.height(22.dp))
      
              Text(
                  text = "HOW IT WORKS",
                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                  color = InkTextTertiary,
                  letterSpacing = 1.4.sp,
              )
      
              Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
      
              listOf(
                  "Only the time Instagram is really on screen counts.",
                  "When the allowance runs out, a strict lock screen appears.",
                  "Your PIN opens Instagram for five minutes.",
                  "Everything resets at midnight; the limit itself stays.",
              ).forEach { line ->
                  Row(modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 3.dp)) {
                      Box(
                          modifier = Modifier
                              .padding(top = 7.dp)
                              .size(3.dp)
                              .clip(CircleShape)
                              .background(InkTextTertiary),
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(9.dp))
                      Text(
                          text = line,
                          style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                          color = InkTextTertiary,
                      )
                  }
              }
      
              if (ready && !notificationsGranted) {
                  Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
                  TextButton(
                      onClick = onRequestNotifications,
                      colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = InkTextSecondary),
                  ) {
                      Icon(
                          imageVector = Icons.Filled.Notifications,
                          contentDescription = null,
                          modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp),
                      )
                      Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                      Text("Enable protection alerts", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge)
                  }
              }
      
          }
      }
      
      /* =============================================================================================
       * Small primitives
       * =========================================================================================== */
      
      @Composable
      private fun SectionLabel(text: String) {
          Text(
              text = text,
              style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
              color = InkTextTertiary,
              letterSpacing = 1.4.sp,
          )
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun Hairline() {
          HorizontalDivider(color = InkHairline)
      }
      
      @Composable
      private fun InlineNotice(
          icon: ImageVector,
          text: String,
          color: Color,
          container: Color,
      ) {
          Row(
              modifier = Modifier
                  .fillMaxWidth()
                  .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                  .background(container)

              Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))

                  style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                  color = color,
              )

    }

    /**
     * Loads persisted state. Safe to call repeatedly; only the first call has an effect.
     */
    fun initialize(context: Context) {
        if (::prefs.isInitialized) return
        prefs = context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val stored = DailyLimit(
            isEnabled = prefs.getBoolean(KEY_LIMIT_ENABLED, false),
            limitMs = prefs.getLong(KEY_LIMIT_MS, 0L),
            usedMs = prefs.getLong(KEY_USED_MS, 0L),
            dayStartWallClock = prefs.getLong(KEY_DAY_START, 0L),
        )
        val rolled = stored.rolledToDay(now)
        _limit.value = rolled

        // The three PIN extensions are per calendar day, just like the usage counter.
        val storedExtensions = prefs.getInt(KEY_PIN_EXTENSIONS_USED, 0)
        _pinExtensionsUsed.value =
            if (stored.dayStartWallClock == rolled.dayStartWallClock) {
                storedExtensions.coerceIn(0, BlockPolicy.MAX_PIN_EXTENSIONS_PER_DAY)
            } else {
                0
            }

        // A running foreground session survives a process death: its start time was persisted.
        val storedSince = prefs.getLong(KEY_USAGE_SINCE, 0L)
        _usageSince.value =
            if (storedSince > 0L) maxOf(storedSince, rolled.dayStartWallClock) else 0L

        val storedSessionEnd = prefs.getLong(KEY_SESSION_END, 0L)
        if (storedSessionEnd in 1 until now) {
            // The session expired while the process was dead - the block is active again.
            _sessionEndWallClock.value = 0L
            prefs.edit { putLong(KEY_SESSION_END, 0L) }
        } else {
            _sessionEndWallClock.value = storedSessionEnd
        }
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------------- usage tracking

    /**
     * Called by the watchdog whenever the foreground app changes.
     *
     * Only a blocked app that is really on screen consumes the allowance; the counter is paused
     * as soon as the user leaves the app and resumes when they come back.
     */
    fun onForegroundAppChanged(packageName: String?) {
        _foregroundPackage.value = packageName
        val now = _now.value
        if (shouldCountUsage(packageName)) startCounting(now) else stopCounting(now)
    }

    /**
     * Called when the lock screen covers (or uncovers) the blocked app.
     *
     * While the lock screen is up the user is not looking at the blocked app, so that time must
....

        _limit.value = rolled
        _pinExtensionsUsed.value = 0
        // A session that started before midnight only counts from the new day onwards.
        val since = _usageSince.value
        if (since > 0L) _usageSince.value = maxOf(since, rolled.dayStartWallClock)
        persist()
        Log.i(TAG, "New day - today's usage and PIN extension counters reset")
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------------- PIN gated actions

    /**
     * Checks the PIN without granting anything.
     *
     * Used by every setting that the user must not be able to change without the PIN.
     */
    fun authorize(pin: String): Boolean = matchesPin(pin)

    /**
     * Validates a PIN entry for the lock screen.
     *
     * @return true when the PIN matched, in which case a five-minute unlock session was started.
     */
    fun submitPin(code: String): Boolean {
        if (!matchesPin(code)) return false
        if (!grantPinExtension(BlockPolicy.UNLOCK_SESSION_MS)) return false

        Log.i(TAG, "Correct PIN - unlock session granted")
        return true
    }

    private fun matchesPin(code: String): Boolean {
        val accepted = BlockPolicy.isCorrectPin(code)
        Log.i(TAG, "PIN check for ${code.length} digits -> ${if (accepted) "accepted" else "rejected"}")
        return accepted
    }

    /**
     * True when the daily allowance has already been configured at least once.
     *
     * The very first setup is deliberately free so the blocker can be armed without knowing the
     * PIN; every later change (including re-arming after switching it off) needs the PIN.
     */
    fun isPinRequiredToSetLimit(): Boolean =
        _limit.value.isEnabled || _limit.value.limitMs > 0L

    /**
     * Sets the daily allowance and switches the limiter on.
     *
     * The allowance stays exactly as set until it is changed again with the PIN.
     *
     * @param pin the PIN; null is only accepted while no allowance has been configured yet.
     * @return true when the allowance was applied (either first setup or a correct PIN).
     */
    fun applyDailyLimit(limitMs: Long, pin: String?): Boolean {
        val firstSetup = !isPinRequiredToSetLimit()
        if (!firstSetup && (pin == null || !authorize(pin))) return false

        val rolled = _limit.value.rolledToDay(_now.value)
        _limit.value = rolled.copy(isEnabled = true, limitMs = limitMs.coerceAtLeast(0L))
        persist()
        Log.i(TAG, "Daily allowance set to ${_limit.value.limitMs} ms (first setup=$firstSetup)")
        return true
    }

    /**
     * Switches the limiter on or off.
     *
     * Switching it off requires the PIN as well - there is no free way to disable the blocker.
     *
     * @return true when the PIN was correct and the limiter was switched.
     */
    fun setLimiterEnabled(enabled: Boolean, pin: String): Boolean {
        if (!authorize(pin)) return false

        if (enabled) {
            _limit.value = _limit.value.copy(isEnabled = true)
        } else {
            stopCounting(_now.value)
            _limit.value = _limit.value.copy(isEnabled = false)
            endSession()
        }
        persist()
        Log.i(TAG, "Limiter enabled=$enabled")
        return true
    }

    /**
     * Clears today's usage so the full allowance is available again.
     *
     * @return true when the PIN was correct and the counter was reset.
     */
    // ----------------------------------------------------------------- unlock session

    /** Starts the temporary unlock session that lifts the block for [durationMs]. */
    private fun grantUnlockSession(durationMs: Long) {
        val end = System.currentTimeMillis() + durationMs
        _sessionEndWallClock.value = end
        prefs.edit { putLong(KEY_SESSION_END, end) }
    }

    /**
     * Temporarily suspends blocking for [durationMs] after verifying the PIN.
     *
     * The pause reuses the unlock session, so usage keeps counting and the block returns as soon
     * as the session ends and the allowance is still exhausted.
     *
     * @return true when the PIN was correct and the pause was granted.
     */
    fun pauseFor(durationMs: Long, pin: String): Boolean {
        if (!authorize(pin)) return false
        if (!grantPinExtension(durationMs)) return false

        Log.i(TAG, "Pause granted for $durationMs ms")
        return true
    }

    /**
     * Grants one of today's three PIN extensions and starts the session.
     *
     * Both the five-minute unlock on the lock screen and the fifteen-minute pause on the
     * dashboard consume one extension, so the PIN cannot be used to keep resetting the timer.
     */
    private fun grantPinExtension(durationMs: Long): Boolean {
        val used = _pinExtensionsUsed.value
        if (used >= BlockPolicy.MAX_PIN_EXTENSIONS_PER_DAY) {
            Log.i(TAG, "No PIN extensions left today")
            return false
        }

        _pinExtensionsUsed.value = used + 1
        grantUnlockSession(durationMs)
        persist()
        Log.i(
            TAG,
            "PIN extension granted (${_pinExtensionsUsed.value}/" +
                "${BlockPolicy.MAX_PIN_EXTENSIONS_PER_DAY} used today)",
        )
        return true
    }

    /** True when at least one of today's three PIN extensions is still available. */
    fun hasPinExtensionsLeft(): Boolean =
        _pinExtensionsUsed.value < BlockPolicy.MAX_PIN_EXTENSIONS_PER_DAY

    /** Immediately terminates any running unlock session, re-arming the block. */
    fun endSession() {
        _sessionEndWallClock.value = 0L
        prefs.edit { putLong(KEY_SESSION_END, 0L) }
    }

      package com.example.shaylock.ui
      
      /**
       * Snapshot of the system permissions the blocker depends on.
       *
       * Refreshed every time the setup screen resumes, because both permissions are granted in
       * separate system screens.
       */
      data class PermissionUiState(
          /** The accessibility watchdog is switched on in settings and connected to the app. */
          val accessibilityEnabled: Boolean = false,
          /**
           * The service is listed as enabled in system settings. It can be true while the service is
           * still reconnecting after a process restart, which the UI shows as "Connecting".
           */
          val accessibilityToggleOn: Boolean = false,
          /** The app may draw over other apps (`SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW`). */
          val overlayGranted: Boolean = false,
          /** The app may post the foreground service notification (Android 13+). */
          val notificationsGranted: Boolean = false,
          /** Instagram is actually installed on this device. */
          val targetInstalled: Boolean = true,
      ) {
          /** The permission is enabled but the system has not connected the service yet. */
          val accessibilityConnecting: Boolean
              get() = accessibilityToggleOn && !accessibilityEnabled
      
          /** Both mandatory permissions are fully in place, so the blocker can actually work. */
          val readyToBlock: Boolean
              get() = accessibilityEnabled && overlayGranted
      }
      
- path: app\src\main\java\com\example\shaylock\core\PermissionUtils.kt
  content: |
      package com.example.shaylock.core
      
      import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityServiceInfo
      import android.app.AppOpsManager
      import android.content.ComponentName
      import android.content.Context
      import android.os.Build
      import android.os.Process
      import android.provider.Settings
      import android.text.TextUtils
      import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityManager
      import com.example.shaylock.service.BlockerAccessibilityService
      
      /**
       * Helpers for querying the two "special" system permissions the blocker needs.
       *
       * Android does not expose a single reliable API for either of them: OEM builds store the
       * accessibility component in slightly different formats and some of them lag behind when the
       * enabled-service list is read. Every check therefore combines the authoritative system API with
       * a parsed fallback from `Settings.Secure` / `AppOpsManager`.
       */
      object PermissionUtils {
      
          /**
           * True only when our [BlockerAccessibilityService] is both switched on in system settings and
           * actually connected/running.
           *
           * A component can stay listed in the settings while the service is not connected (for example
           * right after a process restart). The UI treats that state as "connecting" instead of "ready",
           * so the blocker is never armed without a live watchdog.
           */
          fun isAccessibilityServiceEnabled(context: Context): Boolean =
              isAccessibilityServiceEnabledInSettings(context) && isAccessibilityServiceRunning()
      
          /**
           * True when the service is listed as enabled in the system settings, regardless of whether the
           * service instance has finished connecting. Used by the dashboard to distinguish
           * "Action required" from "Connecting".
           */
          fun isAccessibilityServiceEnabledInSettings(context: Context): Boolean {
              val expected = ComponentName(context, BlockerAccessibilityService::class.java)
      
              // Primary source: the accessibility framework's own enabled-service list.
              val manager = context.getSystemService(Context.ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICE) as? AccessibilityManager
              if (manager != null) {
                  val enabledServices = manager.getEnabledAccessibilityServiceList(
                      AccessibilityServiceInfo.FEEDBACK_ALL_MASK,
                  )
                  if (enabledServices.any { info -> matchesServiceInfo(info, expected) }) {
                      return true
                  }
              }
      
              // Fallback for OEM builds that do not populate the list above reliably.
              val resolver = context.contentResolver
              val accessibilityEnabled = Settings.Secure.getInt(
                  resolver,
                  Settings.Secure.ACCESSIBILITY_ENABLED,
                  0,
              )
              if (accessibilityEnabled != 1) return false
      
              val enabledServices = Settings.Secure.getString(
                  resolver,
                  Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES,
              ) ?: return false
      
              if (TextUtils.isEmpty(enabledServices)) return false
      
              // Components are normally separated by ':', a few OEM builds use ';'.
              return enabledServices
                  .split(':', ';')
                  .any { entry -> matchesFlattenedComponent(entry, expected) }
          }
      
          /** True when the accessibility service instance is currently connected. */
          fun isAccessibilityServiceRunning(): Boolean = BlockerAccessibilityService.isRunning
      
          /** True when the app is allowed to draw over other apps. */
          fun canDrawOverlays(context: Context): Boolean {
              if (Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)) return true
      
              // Fallback: some OEMs return a stale value from Settings but keep the app-op up to date.
              return try {
                  val appOps = context.getSystemService(Context.APP_OPS_SERVICE) as? AppOpsManager
                      ?: return false
                  val mode = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                      appOps.unsafeCheckOpNoThrow(
                          AppOpsManager.OPSTR_SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW,
                          Process.myUid(),
                          context.packageName,
                      )
                  } else {
                      @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                      appOps.checkOpNoThrow(
                          AppOpsManager.OPSTR_SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW,
                          Process.myUid(),
                          context.packageName,
                      )
                  }
                  mode == AppOpsManager.MODE_ALLOWED
              } catch (e: Exception) {
                  false
              }
          }
      
          /** True when Instagram is actually installed on the device. */
          fun isTargetInstalled(context: Context): Boolean = try {
              context.packageManager.getApplicationInfo(BlockPolicy.PRIMARY_PACKAGE, 0)
              true
          } catch (e: Exception) {
              false
          }
      
          private fun matchesServiceInfo(
              info: AccessibilityServiceInfo,
              expected: ComponentName,
          ): Boolean {
              val serviceInfo = info.resolveInfo?.serviceInfo ?: return false
              return serviceInfo.packageName.equals(expected.packageName, ignoreCase = true) &&
                  matchesClassName(serviceInfo.name, expected.className)
          }
      
          private fun matchesFlattenedComponent(entry: String, expected: ComponentName): Boolean {
              val flattened = entry.trim()
              if (flattened.isEmpty()) return false
      
              val parsed = ComponentName.unflattenFromString(flattened) ?: return false
              return parsed.packageName.equals(expected.packageName, ignoreCase = true) &&
                  matchesClassName(parsed.className, expected.className)
          }
      
          /**
           * Compares fully-qualified, short (`.service.Foo`) and bare (`Foo`) class names.
           */
          private fun matchesClassName(actual: String, expected: String): Boolean {
              val actualName = actual.removePrefix(".").trim()
              val expectedName = expected.removePrefix(".").trim()
      
              if (actualName.equals(expectedName, ignoreCase = true)) return true
              if (actualName.endsWith(".$expectedName", ignoreCase = true)) return true
              return expectedName.endsWith(".$actualName", ignoreCase = true)
          }
      }

> _Reasoning_
> The service is bound and enabled; the UI dump may have been from before it connected. Current time is 00:04 on Oct 24, but `day_start` is Oct 23 00:00. The ticker should roll over at midnight and reset the count. But the prefs `pin_extensions_used=1` and `day_start=1792693800000` (Oct 23 00:00) despite it being Oct 24 00:04. Why hasn't the rollover occurred? The app process may have been killed/not running, or the service is running but the ticker didn't update prefs? The `rolloverIfNeeded` calls `persist()` which should update `day_start` to Oct 24. The prefs show old day start. Maybe the app was force-stopped and the service just reconnected; `BlockController.initialize` calls `stored.rolledToDay(now)` and sets `_limit.value = rolled` but **does not persist the rolled day** unless something triggers `persist()`. Ah! In `initialize`, it sets `_limit.value = rolled` but doesn't call `persist()`. If the day rolled while dead, the in-memory day start is today, but prefs still has yesterday's day start.       package com.example.shaylock.service
      
      import android.content.Context
      import android.content.Intent
      import android.provider.Settings
      import android.util.Log
      import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
      
      /**
       * Thin facade used by the accessibility watchdog and the setup UI to drive [OverlayService].
       */
      object OverlayController {
      
          private const val TAG = "OverlayController"
      
          /**
           * Raises the lock screen over the currently visible app.
           *
           * The service is normally already running as a foreground service (it is started when the
           * rule is armed), so this call only delivers a new intent to it.
           */
          fun show(context: Context, targetPackage: String) {
              if (!Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)) {
                  Log.w(TAG, "Overlay permission not granted - cannot block $targetPackage")
                  return
              }
              if (OverlayService.isOverlayShowing) return
      
              val intent = Intent(context, OverlayService::class.java).apply {
                  action = OverlayService.ACTION_SHOW
                  putExtra(OverlayService.EXTRA_PACKAGE, targetPackage)
              }
      
              if (start(context, intent)) return
      
              // The foreground service could not be started (Android 12+ background start restrictions).
              // The overlay window itself only needs the "display over other apps" permission, so it is
              // drawn straight from this context - the blocker must work even without the service.
              Log.w(TAG, "OverlayService unavailable - drawing the lock screen directly")
              OverlayWindowHost.show(context, targetPackage) { /* nothing to stop */ }
          }
      
          /** Lowers the lock screen. */
          fun hide(context: Context) {
              // When the service is already running it can be torn down directly. Going through an
              // intent would start a foreground service only to stop it again.
              val running = OverlayService.instance
              if (running != null) {
                  running.stopEverything()
                  return
              }
      
              val intent = Intent(context, OverlayService::class.java).apply {
                  action = OverlayService.ACTION_HIDE
              }
              start(context, intent)
          }
      
          /**
           * Starts the foreground service so that it is already alive when a block is needed.
           *
           * Called from the foreground (the setup screen) which keeps the foreground service start
           * restriction on Android 12+ satisfied.
           */
          fun keepAlive(context: Context) {
              val intent = Intent(context, OverlayService::class.java).apply {
                  action = OverlayService.ACTION_KEEP_ALIVE
              }
              start(context, intent)
          }
      
          /** Stops the foreground service completely. */
          fun stop(context: Context) {
              val running = OverlayService.instance
              if (running != null) {
                  running.stopEverything()
                  return
              }
      
              val intent = Intent(context, OverlayService::class.java).apply {
                  action = OverlayService.ACTION_STOP
              }
              start(context, intent)
          }
      
          /**
           * Starts the overlay service.
           *
           * @return true when the service was started (or was already running).
           */
          private fun start(context: Context, intent: Intent): Boolean {
              try {
                  ContextCompat.startForegroundService(context, intent)
                  return true
              } catch (e: Exception) {
                  Log.w(TAG, "startForegroundService rejected, falling back to startService", e)
              }
              try {
                  context.startService(intent)
                  return true
              } catch (e: Exception) {
                  Log.e(TAG, "Unable to start OverlayService", e)
                  return false
              }
          }
      }
      
- path: app\src\main\java\com\example\shaylock\service\OverlayService.kt
  content: |
      package com.example.shaylock.service
      
      import android.app.Notification
      import android.app.NotificationChannel
      import android.app.NotificationManager
      import android.app.PendingIntent
      import android.app.Service
      import android.content.Intent
      import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
      import android.os.Build
      import android.os.IBinder
      import android.util.Log
      import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
      import androidx.core.app.ServiceCompat
      import com.example.shaylock.MainActivity
      import com.example.shaylock.R
      import com.example.shaylock.core.BlockController
      
      /**
       * Foreground service that owns the blocking session.
       *
       * It does three things:
       *  1. Keeps the process alive (and visible to the user through a low-key notification) while a
       *     blocking rule is armed.
       *  2. Raises and lowers the full-screen lock window via [OverlayWindowHost].
       *  3. Runs the 5 minute unlock session timer (driven by [BlockController]).
       *
       * A foreground service is used so that the lock screen can be raised while the app itself is in
       * the background, which is the whole point of a blocker.
       */
      class OverlayService : Service() {
      
          override fun onCreate() {
              super.onCreate()
              instance = this
              // The service can be recreated after a process death without the Application object
              // having been recreated in the same order, so make the persisted state available first.
              BlockController.initialize(applicationContext)
              createNotificationChannel()
          }
      
          override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
              // The service is always started through startForegroundService(), so startForeground()
              // must be called within a few seconds - for *every* action, including the ones that
              // immediately tear the service down again. Otherwise the system kills the app with
              // "Context.startForegroundService() did not then call Service.startForeground()".
              try {
                  promoteToForeground()
              } catch (e: Exception) {
                  // Never let a broken notification take the whole blocker down: the overlay window
                  // itself only needs the "display over other apps" permission.
                  Log.e(TAG, "Unable to promote the service to the foreground", e)
              }
      
              when (intent?.action) {
                  ACTION_SHOW -> {
                      val target = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_PACKAGE).orEmpty()
                      OverlayWindowHost.show(this, target) {
                          // Valid PIN: tear the whole service down, the session took over.
                          Log.i(TAG, "Unlocked, stopping overlay service")
                          stopEverything()
                      }
                  }
      
                  ACTION_KEEP_ALIVE -> {
                      Log.i(TAG, "Overlay service alive")
                  }
      
                  ACTION_HIDE -> {
                      OverlayWindowHost.dismiss()
                      stopEverything()
                  }
      
                  ACTION_STOP -> {
                      OverlayWindowHost.dismiss()
                      stopEverything()
                  }
      
                  else -> {
                      // A null intent means Android recreated the service after killing the process.
                      // Ask the watchdog to re-evaluate the app that is on screen right now; the next
                      // accessibility event may otherwise only arrive after the user leaves the app.
                      Log.i(TAG, "Service restarted by the system")
                      BlockerAccessibilityService.instance?.recheckForeground()
                  }
              }
              return START_STICKY
          }
      
          override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null
      
          override fun onDestroy() {
              OverlayWindowHost.dismiss()
              instance = null
              super.onDestroy()
          }
      
          /** Removes the lock window and stops the foreground service. */
          fun stopEverything() {
              OverlayWindowHost.dismiss()
              ServiceCompat.stopForeground(this, ServiceCompat.STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
              stopSelf()
          }
      
          /**
           * Promotes the service to the foreground so the lock screen may be drawn over other apps.
           *
           * On API 34+ the `specialUse` foreground service type is required and declared in the
           * manifest. On older releases the type flag is not understood by the platform, so the
           * two-argument overload is used to avoid an `IllegalArgumentException`.
           */
          private fun promoteToForeground() {
              val notification = buildNotification()
              if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.UPSIDE_DOWN_CAKE) {
                  ServiceCompat.startForeground(
                      this,
                      NOTIFICATION_ID,
                      notification,
                      ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_SPECIAL_USE,
                  )
              } else {
                  startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
              }
          }
      
          private fun buildNotification(): Notification {
              val openApp = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                  this,
                  0,
                  Intent(this, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                      flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
                  },
                  PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
              )
      
              return NotificationCompat.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
                  .setSmallIcon(R.drawable.ic_shaylock_mark)
                  .setContentTitle(getString(R.string.notification_blocking_title))
                  .setContentText(getString(R.string.notification_blocking_text))
                  .setContentIntent(openApp)
                  .setOngoing(true)
                  .setShowWhen(false)
                  .setPriority(NotificationCompat.PRIORITY_LOW)
                  .setCategory(NotificationCompat.CATEGORY_SERVICE)
                  .build()
          }
      
          private fun createNotificationChannel() {
              val manager = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java) ?: return
              if (manager.getNotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID) != null) return
              val channel = NotificationChannel(
                  CHANNEL_ID,
                  getString(R.string.notification_channel_name),
                  NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW,
              ).apply {
                  description = getString(R.string.notification_channel_description)
                  setShowBadge(false)
              }
              manager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
          }
      
          companion object {
              private const val TAG = "OverlayService"
      
              private const val CHANNEL_ID = "shaylock_blocking"
              private const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 4711
      
              const val ACTION_SHOW = "com.example.shaylock.action.SHOW_OVERLAY"
              const val ACTION_HIDE = "com.example.shaylock.action.HIDE_OVERLAY"
              const val ACTION_KEEP_ALIVE = "com.example.shaylock.action.KEEP_ALIVE"
              const val ACTION_STOP = "com.example.shaylock.action.STOP_SERVICE"
              const val EXTRA_PACKAGE = "com.example.shaylock.extra.TARGET_PACKAGE"
      
              @Volatile
              var instance: OverlayService? = null
                  private set
      
              /** True while the lock window is visible on screen. */
              val isOverlayShowing: Boolean
                  get() = OverlayWindowHost.isShowing
          }
      }

          private fun confirmWithPin(request: PinRequest, code: String): Boolean {

- path: app\src\main\java\com\example\shaylock\core\BlockPolicy.kt
  content: |
          const val UNLOCK_SESSION_MS: Long = 5 * 60 * 1000L
      
          /**
           * How long the "Pause" action suspends the block.
           *
           * The pause is deliberately short and PIN-gated: it is an escape hatch, not a way to disable
           * protection for the rest of the day.
           */
          const val PAUSE_DURATION_MS: Long = 15 * 60 * 1000L
      
          /**
           * How many PIN-approved extensions (a 5-minute unlock or a 15-minute pause) are allowed
           * per day. Once all of them are used, the block cannot be lifted again until midnight.
           */
          const val MAX_PIN_EXTENSIONS_PER_DAY: Int = 3
      
          /** True while another PIN extension is still available today. */
          fun canUsePinExtension(usedToday: Int): Boolean =
              usedToday in 0 until MAX_PIN_EXTENSIONS_PER_DAY
      
          /** Convenience predicate used by the watchdog. */
          fun isBlockedPackage(packageName: String?): Boolean =
              packageName != null && BLOCKED_PACKAGES.contains(packageName)
      
          /**
           * Selectable daily allowances offered in the setup UI, in milliseconds.
           *
           * The chosen value is fixed: it stays exactly as selected until it is changed again with the
           * PIN. Only the usage counter is reset, and that happens automatically at local midnight.
           */

> "Done. Reset is gone and PIN access is capped at 3 sessions per day.

> What changed:
> - Dashboard: removed the 'Reset today's usage' action entirely (UI, callback, and BlockController.resetTodayUsage). Usage now only clears at midnight.
> - Added MAX_PIN_EXTENSIONS_PER_DAY = 3, persisted per calendar day (`pin_extensions_used`) and reset with the usage counter at midnight.
> - Every PIN-granted session consumes one: the lock screen's 'Unlock for 5 minutes' and the dashboard's 'Pause for 15 minutes' (so the pause can't be used to bypass the cap).
> - Lock screen shows 'X of 3 PIN extensions left today'; at 0 it shows 'No PIN extensions left today', disables the keypad/unlock, and stays blocked until midnight.
> - Dashboard shows remaining extensions under the pause button; at 0 the pause button is replaced with an 'all 3 used' notice.
> - Allowance chips are now a 2x2 grid (10s, 15m, 30m, 1h) and the earlier logo/shield changes remain.
> 
> Verified:
> - `:app:assembleDebug`, `:app:testDebugUnitTest` (6 tests, including the new 3-per-day boundary test), `:app:lintDebug` — all pass.
> - APK: app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk.
>

> - The best plan depends on the threat model:
>   1. Self-control / friction only: keep one PIN, but don't hardcode it; set it once and store a salted hash; require it for protected actions; add escalating delays, random delays, cooldowns; limit bypass actions.
>   2. Accountability: partner-held code (the removed flow) or a backend; partner approves unlock; separate admin actions require partner. No PIN visible to user; codes single-use/expiring.
>   3. Device-owner security: use Android BiometricPrompt/device credential for admin actions, and store a random secret in Keystore. But biometrics are still the user, so it's authentication, not accountability.
> - Suggested concrete design for ShayLock now:
>   - Keep a single 4-digit "unlock PIN" for the 5-minute extension, but change it from hardcoded to a setup-time PIN stored as a salted PBKDF2 hash (or use a 6-digit PIN). Don't display it anywhere.
